# SUSTENA — RUST BUILD · WBD + TRACKER

*The single source of truth for building **all** Sustena features (every technical article) in Rust. This is both the Work-Breakdown Document (what to build) and the live tracker (what's done / pending). It supersedes the earlier "upgrade the Python app in place" framing and folds in the scattered trackers (R2_BACKLOG, ledgers).*

**Angle:** the engine is being rebuilt as a portable **Rust core** (`sustena-core`), verified against conformance vectors. **R1** re-laid the existing Python behaviour in Rust at parity; **R2** builds the article specs the Python code never had. This document is the full remaining breakdown, article by article, through to the whole system.

> ★ Direction confirmed by Bonnie, 2026-08-12: *"continue with rust build for all sustena features in the technical articles."* The original spec's §0 First Principle (upgrade Python in place, stay in WSL, no rebuild) is **superseded** — recorded for history, not deleted. The Python app stays **live and untouched** as the reference until the Rust core ships.

---

## Status legend

- ✅ **done** — built in Rust and passing conformance vectors (R1 parity or R2 spec)
- 🔨 **in progress**
- ⬜ **pending** — on the plan, not started
- ⏸️ **deferred** — held with the economy layer (ADR-0001 D5, pending legal) or genuinely-later on engineering grounds
- **✅ app-layer** — built and live in the Python reference, not yet in the Rust core (real, but not portable yet)

*Build note: **all 18 technical articles have now been fully read and extracted** — the completeness checklist at the end is all ✓ and this WBD is feature-complete as a breakdown. What remains is the building.*

> **★ Standing rule — read to the last line of the article.** Many articles carry a trailing **`## Additions — ratified 2026-08-05`** section and/or **corrections inside the Sustena Note** (*"that is now superseded"*, *"four corrections to that audit"*). These are **canon**, they routinely **flip a status from not-built to built**, and they are easy to miss by stopping at the references. Rows sourced from them are marked `Add.` or `Note` in the § column.
>
> **Never label anything "not article-derived" without reading the article first.** If it appears in an article, cite the §. Two instances of this error were found and corrected on 2026-08-12, and they had **different failure modes** — worth knowing both:
>
> - **Stopping at the references.** M-PAWA's KES peg, royalty schedule and settlement stack were folded from a retired tracker and labelled "not article-derived"; they are in Pawa's own trailing *Additions* section.
> - **Reading a section but not exhaustively.** ADD-2b's governance model was recorded as "in no article body" on the strength of a `grep` for `RFC` — which only matched *RFC 1034*, the DNS citation. Mycelium **§X·§XI** already carried most of it under different words (Foundation-with-zero-authority, `CouncilSession`/`StakeholderSession`, per-edge privileges, skins, quorum, the FLP/Paxos/BFT bounds). **A keyword miss is not an absence.** Read the section.

ratified royalty flow ✅, PAWA-6 the treasury as a Sustain ✅ PAWA-7/MYC-6 genesis with minting made visible ✅ PAWA-11 governance 🔨 (mechanism ✅, three parameters wired) and PAWA-8 ongoing issuance ✅ in Rust** (`pawa.rs`: the formula at parity with `pawa_meter.py`, reproducible with **no wall-clock in the price**, a refusal **unconstructible** as a reading, κ **declared-uncalibrated**, and **no balance touched at all**). ★★★ The hard boundary (ADR-0001 D5) holds throughout: **internal accounting only** — issuing a real transferable token is a distinct, later, human-authorised act. ★★★ And the ledger's debit is **backed by a measurement structurally** — `charge` takes a `PawaReading`, which cannot be constructed except by metering a committed run, so there is no `debit(amount)` to invent one. ★★★ And the gate now carries `balance ≥ pawa` as its last conjunct, **priced against the candidate's real cost** — which the gate's own effect-on-a-copy makes free — so an unaffordable run is refused **before it costs anything**, while `Affordability::Unmetered` keeps every pre-economy caller byte-identical. ★★★ And the **ratified five-way royalty** now settles as a **conserved internal transfer** — `ΣΔ = 0` by the shape of the entry, an absent role's share folded into the treasury rather than dropped, and **no juul→real path anywhere**. ★★★ And the **treasury is now a real Sustain `Σ_T`** — a `Definition` with `V_T`, whose reserve floor is an **ordinary invariant** the existing gate refuses a breach of, whose outflows are **conserved transfers** through the same `execute_admitted`, and every one of them **human-gated by an approval token**. **No new machinery: a treasury is governed exactly as a household is.** ★★★ And **genesis** now declares where the juul came from: `Entry::Mint` is a **distinct, visible variant** naming its declaration, `from_genesis` is a **constructor** so genesis is **one-time by shape**, and `circulation = Σ(mints) − Σ(costs)` with transfers at zero. ★★ **Minting the internal unit is not issuing a coin** — the real-coin line is untouched. ★★★ And the **parameters are now a declared Sustain**: a change is a **gated, recorded, replayable Enzyme**, bounds are **ordinary invariants**, and `pawa::compute_pawa` was **deleted** so **nothing prices from a constant** — `κ` is governed end to end. ★★★ And **ongoing issuance** now closes the loop: new juul enters only by **rewarding served work** (`issue` takes a `PawaReading`, so there is **no issuance without work**), at a rate that moves **only** through the gated Enzyme, into the **same visible `Entry::Mint`** — with `circulation = Σ(genesis) + Σ(issuance) − Σ(costs)` asserted as an equation, and **issuance OFF at genesis** so an economy never starts inflating by default. ★★★ And **the seam is closed**: a committed, metered run that opts into `serving` **charges the caller and issues to the server from the same reading** — one `if let` in the block that already held the reading, opt-in and visible at the call site, with a non-serving run byte-identical to before. **The economy layer's named pieces are all built.** Still ⏸️ downstream: **multi-host reward distribution (PAWA-9)**, the **royalty schedule's** own governance (it needs a sum-to-100 bound), and publish is a second write path |
| Cross-cutting | **Capstone** | Capstone · GAIA | ✅ the assembly map — 4 of the gate's 6 duties banked structurally in Rust |

*(Legend for the three SPINE flags: the master spec's operational spine is Monitor→Tenet→Controller — the system's observe / simulate-optimise / govern-decide depth. **All three now read ✅** (2026-08-16): **Monitor ✅ · Tenet ✅ · Controller ✅**, with Tenet's ensemble layer closing the last of the three cores plus the layer above it. **As of 2026-08-16 they are wired into one cycle** (`ooda.rs`, CTL-3) rather than three modules that could be. What remains is the surrounding assembly — the `MonitorEngine` that owns the detection chain per sustain, holarchy escalation, panels — not the mathematics and no longer the loop. This was the highest-attention gap on this document; it is now an assembly job.)*

*An index over rows that already carry full detail below — the agreed order of attack, not a re-description. Row IDs are the contract; read the rows for what each one is.*

**Phase 1 — Foundation.** The substrate the rest stands on.
- ✅ **Operator: OP-3 DONE 2026-08-17** — the static author-time obligation ships as a flag, not a gate term (`obligation.rs`). OP-5 was already ✅.
- **Events & Time substrate:** ~~**EVT-6** (watermarks + per-window lateness)~~ ✅, ~~**EVT-11** (periods, `RRULE` recurrence)~~ ✅ mechanism, ~~**EVT-12** (replay + checkpointing)~~ ✅ — all 2026-08-17. ~~**EVT-10** (dimension-kind typing — snapshot vs accumulating; ties to M-DSL)~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-17** — **the block's attack list is complete.** Adjacent and open in the same layer: **EVT-1** (🔨 — six of seven fields since 2026-08-17; only `payload` remains), **EVT-14**, **EVT-15** (⬜ — blocks M-TEN and M-EDIT). **EVT-5 ✅**, **EVT-6 ✅**, **EVT-11's mechanism ✅**, **EVT-12 ✅** and **EVT-10 ✅** all closed 2026-08-17; EVT-11 stays 🔨 only for the rest of the RRULE grammar.
- **The firewall `F` — one predicate, two homes:** **CON-1** (🔨 — `C` ✅, `D` ✅, **`F` ⬜**) and **CON-2**'s standing *"`F` pending"*; **IMM-1** closes on the same term. ✅ **DONE 2026-08-17** — **SUS-7**, **SUS-8** and **OP-10** closed (`boundary.rs`), then **CON-1**, **CON-2**'s `F` term and **IMM-1**'s `F` closed on top of it (`flow.rs`). The boundary block of Phase 1 is complete. **OP-3 ✅ done 2026-08-17** — the Operator layer has no open rows but metering. **Remaining in Phase 1: the Events & Time substrate rows** (EVT-6/11/12/10, plus the adjacent open EVT-1/14/15). **EVT-5 ✅ done 2026-08-17** (`clocks.rs`) — the two clocks, the skew between them, and the negative reading surfaced and structurally unclampable; `t_ingest` + `source` landed additively on the record, taking **EVT-1 to six of its seven fields** (`payload` is the named residual — it lives on `EmittedEvent` and never reaches the durable `Event`). **EVT-6 ✅ done 2026-08-17** (`watermark.rs`) — *when may I stop waiting* now has a real answer: a window closes only on a watermark, `W(τ)=τ` is available only by declaring a zero bound explicitly, and the lateness policy is mandatory because a heuristic will sometimes be wrong. **EVT-11's mechanism ✅ done 2026-08-17** (`period.rs`) — recurrence, zone-id anchoring and store-the-rule all shipped; its residual is the rest of the RRULE grammar. **EVT-12 ✅ done 2026-08-17** (`checkpoint.rs`) — checkpoints `(s_k,k)`, `O(n−k)` countable, discardability and derived-never-authored both structural, and replay structurally unable to issue an external effect. **EVT-10 ✅ done 2026-08-17** (`dimension.rs`) — LWW attaches from the declaration, and delta-accumulation on a snapshot dimension is a **type error proven by a `compile_fail` doctest**, with a schema-level refusal for the declared-in-data half. ★★ **PHASE 1's ATTACK LIST IS COMPLETE.** Boundary (`SUS-7`/`SUS-8`/`OP-10`), firewall (`CON-1`/`CON-2`/`IMM-1`), Operator (`OP-3`/`OP-5`) and the Events & Time substrate (`EVT-5`/`EVT-6`/`EVT-11`/`EVT-12`/`EVT-10`) all closed on 2026-08-17. **What remains in the layer is declared residual rather than unstarted work**, and is listed as such: EVT-11's RRULE-grammar remainder + the EVT-10 DSL authoring surface (both **Phase 4 / M-DSL** territory), EVT-1's `payload`, EVT-14 effect journaling, and EVT-15 replay-under-`D′` (which **blocks M-TEN and M-EDIT**, so it is the one to schedule deliberately rather than let drift). **→ Phase 2 (Cognition assembly) is next.**

**Phase 2 — Cognition assembly.** The mathematics is done; this is wiring.

- **Controller:** ~~**CTL-11** (damping)~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`damping.rs`) — the frequency-domain face, which separates a loop that settles from one that rings while passing Lyapunov at every step. ★ **CTL-6 (IoT bridge) RELOCATED to a host-integration pass** — ADR-0001 keeps I/O out of the core, and the core-side half (*a device signal becomes a typed event*) is already the ingest door, so it is a declared-port/host-boundary slice rather than a mechanism. ★ **CTL-8 (persistent-panel invariant) MOVED to Phase 3** as a surface row, on the same argument that moved MON-7 — its real consumer is the Curated UI's attention budget. **CTL-9**'s host driver stays outside core by design.

★★★ **PHASE 2 IS COMPLETE (2026-08-17).** The Monitor block closed with MON-8 → MON-13 → MON-4 → MON-1, and the Controller block closes with CTL-11 now that CTL-6 and CTL-8 have found their right homes. **What remains in the cognition layer is declared residual rather than unstarted work**, and is listed as such: MON-3's Metrics/Traces (its Logs third is already ✅ via the event log), and — ~~the **`MonitorEngine` consolidation wiring**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-17**, discharging that residual on all four rows at once, and finding a real seam on the way (a season-suppressed detection was still being handed to OBSERVE). **→ Phase 3 (Surface + Immune) is next**, and it has gained two rows on the way here: **MON-7** (preattentive encoder) and **CTL-8** (persistent panels), both surface rows whose consumer is the Curated UI.

**Phase 4 — DSL.** ★ **Opened 2026-08-29.** ~~**DSL-1**'s type-check half~~ ✅ and ~~**DSL-5**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29** — taken together because they are one tree-walk: the judgment and the door it guards. ★★ It found that `editing::typecheck` **bound names without typing anything**, so `label >= 5` loaded cleanly and came back from the gate as a violated law. ~~**DSL-8**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29** — `Def(θ)` as a typed function, with the substitution lemma asserted rather than assumed. ~~**DSL-9**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29**. ~~**DSL-10**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29**. ~~**DSL-12**~~ ✅ **mostly done 2026-08-29** (residual: `imports`/versioning, which has no mechanism yet). ~~**DSL-13**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29**. ~~**DSL-14**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29**. ~~**DSL-15**~~ ✅ **done 2026-08-29**. ★★★ **PHASE 4 IS COMPLETE** — every DSL row closed on 2026-08-29 (DSL-1/5 → 8 → 9 → 10 → 12 → 13 → 14 → 15). The one declared residual is DSL-12's `imports` + enforced versioning, which has no mechanism anywhere yet. *Note: the predicate-grammar gaps the snapshot names — arithmetic and dynamic bracket indexing — have no row of their own; they sit under **DSL-1** and were disclosed as spec findings, not invented for this phasing.*

**✅ DOUBLE-BACK — CLOSED 2026-08-17.** All four rows shipped in sequence (OPV-4 → OPV-7 → OPV-11 → OPV-6), and keeping them as one bundle was the right call: each later row consumed the earlier ones directly — `Π` became the meme, `attention` became `M_self`'s budget, `M_world` supplied the divergence trigger, and `moves_within` carried Proposition 3 from `Shared`'s minting all the way to a *learned* self-model in one unbroken chain of types. *Original note, kept:* **deferred, and deliberately as one deep bundle.** **OPV-4** (`Π`, the operator-DAG) plus the rows that only mean anything once it exists: **OPV-6** (learning / `vary`·`select`·`retain`), **OPV-7** (attention), **OPV-11** (`M_self` / `M_world`). Splitting them would mean building three consumers of a thing that is not there.
- ★ **Return trigger, recorded so it is not left to memory:** Phases 1–4 cleared → **the deep bundle is the immediate next work**; **OR** any shallow row turns out to genuinely depend on `Π` → **do `Π` then, not later**. The second clause is the one that matters — a discovered dependency is a signal, not an inconvenience to route around.

**⏸️ PARKED — standing decision, documented rather than forgotten.** The whole **Economy layer** (`M-PAWA`, and `MYC-5`'s treasury/settlement schedule with it). Not sequenced into any phase above; it re-enters only by an explicit decision to un-park it.

## The WBD — by layer, in dependency order

*Dependency order (from the capstone §9.1): Events & Time → DSL → Sustain-design → Operator → Constraint → Ingest → Monitor → (Curated UI ∥ Tenet → Operative → Multiparty); Editing after the operator path; Controller threads through from the first gate check onward.*

### Delivery track — the Modeling Studio (A–F)

*Ratified 2026-08-05, folded in from the retired `SUSTENA_WBD.md`. The layer order above says what to build; this says in what order it reaches a person. The phone collapses to the **capture edge**; the desktop Studio grows to cover **every** engine (Monitor / Control / Simulate / Edit / Network / Complexity) — the Capstone's "every engine in one frame". The `/devui` front end is database-first, the pattern the Curated-UI article identifies as what killed VOS: **rebuild the surface, keep the engine.***

| | Phase | Covers |
|---|---|---|
| **A** | Authoring foundations | rich dimension types (file, url, list-of-records), Enzyme authoring, typed invariants, the config layer — **the Amber-generality track**: declare a structurally-unlike Sustain with zero engine changes |
| **B** | Studio shell + visual structural editor + code hatch | `π : D → G` (= **EDIT-13**). Adopts the preserved slice-1 shell |
| **C** | Agentic modeling assistant | generate-and-verify, **bounded by the types and the gate** (= OPV-23's proposal-distribution posture) |
| **D** | **Operative layer PROPER** | the article's ten: utility vectors, DAG memes, gated fork+sim, council with declared weights, orchestrator, two attentions, Pareto frontier, Goodhart guard, **approval token**, M_self / M_world (= **all of M-OPV**; today's operatives are a stub of this) |
| **E** | Capture edge | strip non-capture phone UI; wire the native listener/parser (= M-ING) |
| **F** | The Library | the meme/artefact library (= OPV-5/6, M-PAWA/Arena) |

**UI principles (Bonnie's design standard, ratified):** form follows function — novelty only when functional; simplify through **navigation, not clutter** (accordions, hidden sidebars, tabs, clear CTAs, pagination — not a wall of cards); **one view, one job** (a view is the answer to one question); progressive disclosure over mega-windows; **"why am I seeing this?" answerable on demand**; stunning, not dumbed down.

**Gate-1, the acceptance milestone:** a stranger uses Sustena on real data for **30 days**. (M3 = declare Amber with zero engine changes; M4 = a non-technical person productive without Bonnie explaining anything.)

### Layer 1 — SUBSTRATE

#### M-EVT · Events & Time  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — Events and Time - Formal Execution.md` (RECORD · GAIA). The append-only log everything folds over; state = fold(events).*

| # | Feature (from the article) | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| EVT-1 | **Event record** `⟨id, type, payload, t_event, t_ingest, source, causes⟩` — immutable, append-only, ordered | I | 🔨 **six of seven fields, 2026-08-17** — `t_ingest` and `source` now ship on `Event` (`event.rs`), both `Option` + `#[serde(default)]` so a pre-slice wire record still deserialises, orders, dedupes and folds byte-identically (asserted against a literal old-shape JSON, not assumed). `Source` is `⟨id, kind ∈ {connector,device,enzyme,human}, trust⟩` — §I's *"which connector, which device, which Enzyme, at what trust level"*. ★ **Two corrections to this row's own prior text, found by reading the code before building:** `causes` was **already present** (it shipped with EVT-8's vector clocks and is the DAG they derive from) — the row was stale, not the code, and it was not re-added; and `payload` was listed as present but **is not on `Event`**. ★ **The honest residual:** `payload` lives on `EmittedEvent {name, payload}` at execution time and **never reaches the durable record** — there is no `From<EmittedEvent> for Event` and nothing bridges them, because minting the id and stamping the two clocks is host territory. Adding an unwritten `payload` field to close the row on paper would be a field nobody populates, so the row stays 🔨 with the gap named. |
| EVT-2 | **Stable intrinsic `id`** (M-Pesa code, not minted UUID) | I·V | ✅ (dedupe keys on the stable id) |
| EVT-3 | **Dot-protocol `type`** `event.<domain>.<type>` (3+ segments); dispatch on declared type, never raw payload | I | ✅ (carried from the Python contract) |
| EVT-4 | **State = fold(apply, s0, L)** — reproducibility; incrementality (cache == rebuild is a testable law) | II | ✅ (R1 event-fold; `rebuild==get_state` proven). **Note: §4J.2's *"❌ State is not a fold"* is SUPERSEDED** — the fold is the write path, not a diagnostic. Two details the Note pins down: the fold is **patch-level, not semantic** (an opaque `{op,path,old,new}` produced while the Enzyme ran, so replay *cannot* diverge from what the Enzyme did — the honest cost is that replay-under-`D′` is a **named, sequenced debt**, = EVT-15/EDIT-11); and all of a call's mutations attach to the **first** published event, so **the fold's true unit is the Enzyme call, not the domain event** |
| EVT-5 | **Two clocks + skew** `= t_ingest − t_event ≥ 0`; skew is normal/unbounded/heavy-tailed; **negative skew surfaced, never clamped**; backfill marked `provenance='legacy'` | III | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`clocks.rs`). `skew_of` is one subtraction — **no threshold, no rejection, no error type**, so §III's week-long forwarding-macro outage is read exactly like a millisecond of skew, and a week-late event still orders back into its own past by `t_event`. ★★ **Negative skew is surfaced and structurally unclampable:** `Skew::Observed` carries a **signed** `i64`, `skew_of` is the only constructor and floors nothing, and `clock_findings` takes **`&[Event]`** — an immutable slice — so writing a corrected time back is *not expressible from the surfacing path*. The clamp is unspellable rather than refused, the same discipline that made a one-sided clamp on a conserved dimension unrepresentable in `flow.rs`. ★ **Three readings, two of them deliberately not numbers:** `Observed(ms)` · `Inferred` (a backfilled row whose two clocks read equal — the arithmetic is 0 and *means nothing*) · `Unknown` (no arrival ever recorded). `Skew::millis()` returns `Option<i64>`, so neither can decay into a `0` a caller averages in as a punctual arrival. ★ Which clock is wrong is a **provenance** question — `ClockFinding::suspect()` reads the source's declared `Trust`: authoritative → the receiver, merely-reporting → the device, absent/derived → `Undetermined`, a real third answer. That is the one consumer of `Trust`, so it is a field with a reader rather than a field with a plan. **Note on the row's own formula:** the `≥ 0` in §III's display equation is the *normal case*, not a constraint — §III's third consequence requires the negative reading to survive, and it does. *Backfill:* `Event::backfilled` sets `t_ingest` from the legacy row's single stamp as an **observation** and infers `t_event` from it under `Provenance::Legacy`, so the marker attaches to the half that was inferred and the observed half is not disowned. |
| EVT-6 | **Watermarks** `W(τ)` monotone; perfect vs heuristic; **per-window lateness policy** ∈ {drop, late-fire, accumulate-and-retract}; no window closes by wall-clock hope | IV | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`watermark.rs`). ★★ **No window closes by wall-clock hope, structurally:** `Window::is_closed_by` and `WindowState::close` take a **`&Watermark` and nothing else** — there is no `close_at(tau)` overload and no `From<i64>`, so a caller holding only a processing time cannot ask the question. A watermark has exactly two origins: a **declared source guarantee** or an **estimate over observed skew**. ★★ That does not make `W(τ) = τ` impossible — a genuinely in-order source has a zero bound — it makes it **sayable only by saying it**: reproducing §4J.3's failure now requires declaring `SourceGuarantee { max_out_of_orderness: 0 }`, the assertion *this source never delivers out of order*, in as many words. A conformance case runs both guarantees at the same τ over the same window: the zero bound closes, the honest 2h bound does not. **The failure stops being a default and becomes a signature.** ★ **Monotonicity structural** — the mark is private, `WatermarkTracker::advance` is the only mover, and a retreating proposal returns `Advance::HeldBack` (reported, not swallowed); a test shows why the requirement exists rather than restating it: a retreat would un-close a window that had already answered. **Explicitly distinguished from EVT-5's clamp rule** — flooring a *measurement* hides a fact; non-decreasing **is the definition** of this object, and the rejected proposal is surfaced. ★ **Heuristic estimated from EVT-5's skew, never invented:** a declared percentile of the observed skews, reporting percentile/allowance/sample-size. `Unknown` and `Inferred` readings contribute nothing (they are not zero skew), and with no observed skew at all it returns `NoObservedSkew` rather than a zero bound — **an unmeasured source is not a punctual one**. ★★ And **no volume of data promotes an estimate**: `estimate_heuristic` can only ever return `Heuristic`, at 3 samples or 500, because heavy-tailed skew means the largest ever seen is not a bound on the next. ★ **The lateness policy is MANDATORY, not optional** — `Window::new` takes a `Lateness` and the type implements no `Default`, so the compiler stops a window existing without one. That is the honest completion of a heuristic: you cannot promise the bound holds, so you must say what you do when it does not. Each cost is real in the type: **only `AccumulateAndRetract` retains its emitted value**, so a `Drop` window cannot issue a retraction even by mistake, and a dropped fact still lands in the late log — dropping it from the *answer* is not pretending it never arrived. |
| EVT-7 | **Exactly-once EFFECT** = at-least-once delivery ∘ idempotent apply; **uniform dedupe on the stable id at the substrate** (repeat = no-op, never raise) | V | ✅ (R2 #18 uniform dedupe) |
| EVT-8 | **Causal order** — happens-before; **Lamport logical clock**; **vector clocks** for concurrency detection (biconditional, O(n)); `t_event` ≠ causal clock, both needed | VI | ✅ **complete 2026-08-16** — the Lamport `(t_event,id)` tie-break shipped in R2 #17; **vector clocks and genuine concurrency detection now ship in `vclock.rs`** (= MUL-6), derived from the `causes` graph the log already records. §VI's *`t_event` is a physical stamp and is NOT a causal clock — both are needed* is kept structurally: `Stamped` carries both, `order_key()` answers *which reading is newer*, the vector clock answers *did this depend on that*, and a test asserts a concurrent pair stays concurrent across a 999,999-unit physical gap — **a huge time gap is not causal evidence** |
| EVT-9 | **Convergence theorem** — snapshot dim `x=(v,τ)`, LWW register on total order `(τ,id)`; join-semilattice → **invariant under permutation & repetition** (order-independent replay; strong eventual consistency) | VII | ✅ (R2 #17 converging LWW join) — **and §VII's own honest limit is now addressed** (2026-08-16): *"two genuinely concurrent edits to the same field under LWW means one is silently discarded"*. With concurrency detectable, `vclock::merge` returns a `MergeResolution` whose `Concurrent` variant **carries both values**, and `Dimension` declares which reading applies — `Snapshot` collapses by the physical order (correct, and what the theorem is scoped to), `Contested` keeps both. The theorem is unchanged; what changed is that its scope is now checkable rather than remembered |
| EVT-10 | **Dimension kind declared** (snapshot vs accumulating) → LWW attaches automatically and **delta-accumulation on a snapshot dim is a type error**; honest limit: LWW scoped to observations, contested edits → §4B merge-CRDTs | VII→DSL | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`dimension.rs`). ★★ **The type error is real and PROVEN BY A `compile_fail` DOCTEST** — `SnapshotDim` carries one that calls `.add(...)` on a balance read off an SMS, and `cargo test --doc` executes it, so the claim is discharged by the code genuinely failing to build. There is no `add` on the type: the mistake is **unspellable**, not caught. ★ **And the honest completion:** a kind declared in a *spec* with an update arriving off a *wire* cannot be a Rust type error, so `DimensionSchema::check` refuses it there too — with the reason travelling along (*not idempotent, so a redelivered delta would be a wrong balance*). Both halves are needed; claiming the compile-time one covered the declared-in-data case would have been the dishonest version. ★★ **Derived, not decreed, and both halves measured:** naive addition's non-idempotence is run as arithmetic (`x+2δ ≠ x+δ`), and made safe it *is* the grow-only counter — a redelivered delta leaves the state **byte-identical**, at the price of an applied-id map that reaches 200 entries after 200 deltas, against LWW's constant **1** after 500 readings. §VII's cost argument as two numbers rather than a sentence. ★ **LWW attaches from the declaration** — the caller declares `snapshot` and never chooses the apply; a stale reading arriving last does not clobber. Convergence is proven with `crdt.rs`'s own `converges`, which folds **every** permutation and then every permutation again with each update **delivered twice**. ★★ **And §VII's emphatic limit is kept structural:** the three kinds are distinct, `Contested` refuses automatic apply **entirely** (any auto-apply is a machine deciding which human was right) and is backed by `crdt.rs`'s OR-set, so the same two concurrent edits keep **both** as contested and exactly **one** as snapshot. LWW cannot eat a contested edit. ★ **Reconciled, not forked:** `vclock::Dimension` gained the third variant rather than a parallel enum being invented; that forced `MergeResolution::resolve` open, which turned a bare `Vec` meaning two things into a typed `Resolved::{Superseded, BothCount, NeedsReconciliation}` — three call sites corrected in place. The `(τ,id)` tie-break is `Observation`'s existing one, reused. **Residual:** the **DSL authoring surface** for declaring a kind is M-DSL's (Phase 4), and `DimensionSchema` is **not yet wired into `State`/`StateAccessor`**, so nothing yet forces an operator through it — both named rather than claimed. |
| EVT-11 | **Periods** — half-open `[a,b)` windows partition time; **RFC-5545 `RRULE` recurrence** + `DTSTART;TZID` anchored to a **zone id** (not a fixed offset); one period primitive, many consumers | VIII | 🔨 **the mechanism is ✅ done 2026-08-17 (`period.rs`); the residual is the rest of the RRULE grammar.** §VIII's *"same object seen twice"* is now complete in one place: EVT-6 built `W ≥ b`, this builds `[a,b)`, and `Period` **wraps EVT-6's `Window`** rather than inventing a second interval type — so a consumer that can close a window on a watermark needs nothing new, and EVT-6's mandatory `Lateness` rides through from the rule. ★★ **The partition is structural, not checked:** the expansion emits `[occ[i], occ[i+1])`, so each period's end **is** the next one's start and a gap or overlap is unrepresentable. Dijkstra (EWD831) at the roll-up point: **a year is the sum of its twelve months, not the months plus twelve boundary instants** — asserted by summing durations against the span AND by checking every boundary lands in exactly one month. ★★ **Store the rule, recompute the expansion:** `Recurrence` holds **no instants** — no field for them, no constructor accepting them, verified through serialisation. And the reason is run rather than described: **one rule under two tzdata releases gives two different instants** while the local time is identical, so a persisted expansion would keep the older answer with nothing to detect it. ★★ **Zone id, not a bare offset:** `Anchor::Zone` resolves through the host's tz data and every period records the `tzdata_version` that derived it; `Anchor::FixedOffset` is kept — real data arrives carrying only an offset — as **a distinct lesser thing** that consults no tz data and carries `tzdata_version: None`. The trap is shown, not warned about: it agrees with the zone today and diverges under the revised release. ★ **The tz data is INJECTED, not bundled** (`TzProvider`), and §VIII's own argument is why — a bundled database pins one release *invisibly*, hiding exactly the versioning the section warns about. The line is drawn where the versioning is: civil-calendar arithmetic is a fixed algorithm and is computed (Hinnant, round-trip tested); timezone offsets are versioned data and are only ever asked for. **No new crate dependency** (`Cargo.toml` unchanged). ★ **DST exercised where it is not vacuous:** Nairobi observes none, so a Europe/Berlin counterparty gives a real **23-hour** spring-forward day and a **25-hour** fall-back day, both still partitioning exactly; a nonexistent local start is refused and an ambiguous one resolves by a **declared** `DstPolicy` (no `Default`, same discipline as `Lateness`). ★★ **The honest residual — the RRULE grammar is partial.** `FREQ=DAILY|WEEKLY|MONTHLY`, `INTERVAL`, `BYMONTHDAY`, `BYDAY` ship, with RFC-5545's skip-invalid-dates rule honoured (31 Jan → Mar → May, February skipped rather than clamped). **`COUNT`, `UNTIL`, `BYSETPOS`, `FREQ=YEARLY|HOURLY`, `BYMONTH`, `BYWEEKNO`, `BYYEARDAY`, `WKST`, `EXDATE`/`RDATE` and multi-value BY- lists do not**, nor does an `RRULE:` **text parser** (the rule is a typed value). And **no consumer has been migrated onto it** — budgets and Monitor could read it and do not yet, so this is a primitive that is ready rather than one in use. |
| EVT-12 | **Replay + checkpointing** `(s_k,k)`, cost O(n−k), always discardable | IX | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`checkpoint.rs`). `fold(s₀,L) = fold(s_k, L[k+1..n])` — §II's homomorphism — verified at **every** `k` from 1 to n, not one convenient one. ★★ **Always discardable, structurally:** `replay` takes `Option<&Checkpoint>`, so the fast path and the from-scratch path are the **same function** and discarding is passing `None` — there is no separate slow implementation that could drift. ★★ **Derived, never authored:** `Checkpoint::at` is the only constructor and it *folds the log*; there is no constructor taking a state, so a checkpoint claiming a state the log never produced is not writable. `events_applied()` makes the `O(n−k)` claim **countable** (2 instead of 5, in a test). ★ **The cheap/expensive split is stated rather than blurred:** attachment is checked in `O(1)` by the event id the checkpoint was taken through — catching the realistic failure, a checkpoint on the wrong log — while only `verify()` re-folds and catches a state corrupted afterwards, at `O(k)`, exactly what the checkpoint saved. Both offered; neither pretended to be the other. ★★ **Replay cannot reach an effect channel:** §IX's `ASSERT no_external_effects_were_issued()` is a fact about the signature, not a check at the end — `replay` takes `&[Event]`, returns a `Value`, and has no sink, no movement channel, no registry and no `execute` in reach. The contrast is the proof: an operator body receives `&mut Vec<EmittedEvent>` and `&mut Vec<Movement>` because emitting is what it is for. ★★ **A finding about §IX's own pseudocode:** `seen = set()` is per call, so ids folded into the checkpoint never enter it — a **late duplicate of an early event is skipped from scratch and applied from a checkpoint** (380 vs 100), which quietly breaks the same section's discardability claim. Carrying the folded id set would make the checkpoint grow with `k`, which is most of what it was for, so the fix is taken where §V already puts it: `replay` requires a substrate-deduped log and refuses otherwise. A conformance case runs the pseudocode **literally** to show both answers. ★ `replay_to` answers §IX's first consumer (Tenet's *stand in a chosen future*); a checkpoint past the point asked for is **ignored, not an error** — the log is always sufficient. |
| EVT-13 | **Purity of `apply`** — no ambient clock / randomness / I/O | IX | ✅ structural — the Rust core is pure; identity + time come from the host |
| EVT-14 | **External effects journaled as their own events** (decision-event + fact-of-sent event; apply never sends; no re-fire on replay) | IX | ✅ **DONE — the status was stale, not the code** (same class as IMM-7). `core/effect_journal.rs` already carried all four clauses: `Decided`/`Sent`/`Failed` as separate journal entries, **`apply_can_send()` returning `false` as an executable claim rather than a comment** (a fold applies opaque mutations; there is no channel in that path), and no-re-fire structurally rather than by a guard. **Verified 29 Aug**, and the verification found a real contradiction with the newly-landed ING-11 module — see `fix/retry-is-a-decision`: this file said a retry always required a person, which conflates the AUDIT rule (a retry is never invisible) with the AUTHORITY rule (who may decide it). Both fixed; `egress::retry_must_be_journalled` now names the obligation explicitly. |
| EVT-15 | **General replay** consumed by **Tenet** (stand in a future) and **Editing** (replay under D′) | IX | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`semantic.rs`) — **Phase 3 opens here.** ★★ **The premise was checked in the code before anything was built on it, and all three parts held:** (1) the patch fold **is** `D`-independent — `checkpoint::replay` takes a log and a checkpoint and there is **no parameter through which a definition could enter**, which is a *property* rather than a shortfall and is precisely why the semantic layer sits **beside** it; (2) ★ the ingredient exists **in the reference, not here** — `operators_log` carries `operator_name` + `input_json` per call and the Rust `Event` carries neither, so `EnzymeCall` is defined here as the addition the reducer cannot exist without; (3) ★★ **`D′` needed no new type** — `editing::Definition` is already `⟨schema, invariants, operators⟩`, the gate's own three inputs, so replaying *under* `D′` is the same `execute` with a different `Definition`. ★★ **Same `D` reproduces; `D′` reinterprets.** The consistency check is asserted (re-running the calls lands where the patches did — without it every `D′` verdict would be about a different history), and under a `D′` that bites, the answer is **partly** inadmissible with the verdict pointed at the specific call: a floor of 1500 leaves the income admissible (1000→1500 clears it exactly) and refuses the allocation (→1200). `NotPermitted` is kept **separate** from `Refused`, because the allow-list and the gate answer different questions and collapsing them would tell an editor to fix the wrong thing. ★★ **Replay emits nothing, guaranteed twice over:** structurally this core has no sink, no egress and no send — **EVT-14 is not built in Rust**, stated rather than assumed away — and explicitly, `ReplayMode::Replay` **discards and counts** every emission, so the guarantee stays legible once an effect layer exists. `Live` must be *named*, the same discipline `Authorization::Unchecked` follows. ★ **Purity:** `is_deterministic` runs the calls twice and compares — which detects non-determinism and **does not prove purity**, stated as a limit rather than left to be discovered. ★ **Tenet's consumer was already served** by EVT-12 + the simulator; the genuinely new work is the Editing one. **Residuals:** the call log is **not wired to `execute`** (nothing yet records an `EnzymeCall` when an operator runs) — ★★★ **HALF DISCHARGED 2026-08-18 (follow-on wiring 3, `enzyme.rs`): `EnzymeCall` NOW HAS A PRODUCER.** Every one in the crate had been hand-built in a test; `propose_call` builds them from a described effect over the real `Definition` + `Registry`, and a proposed call is handed straight to `replay_under` and comes back `Admitted` — so the reducer finally consumes something it was not passed by hand. ★★ **The other half is named, not claimed:** this residual's own wording asks for a **recorder** (`execute` writing a call log), and that is **not** built, because the log is **host state** — the same call the ING rows made about capture history. So: *producer ✅, recorder ⬜*; `boundary`/`firewall` are not part of a `Definition`, so a `D′` replay checks schema/invariants/allow-list and not `μ` or `F`; and EVT-12's checkpoints do not transfer, since a checkpoint under `D` is not one under `D′`. |

**Summary:** the *convergence core* — fold, event-time LWW, dedupe, purity — is ✅ in Rust (the hardest part, already done). **Update 2026-08-16: the two-clock split is closed** (`vclock.rs`, EVT-8 = MUL-6). §VI's *`t_event` is a physical stamp and is NOT a causal clock — both are needed* is now structural: `Stamped` carries both, and they answer different questions. Vector clocks restore the biconditional the scalar clock cannot, and the gain is concrete rather than theoretical — the scalar answer reports a **false concurrent** across a transitive chain that crosses a node boundary, and a conformance case runs both answers side by side. That also sharpens **EVT-9 at the exact point §VII flags against itself**: *"two genuinely concurrent edits to the same field under LWW means one is silently discarded"* — with concurrency detectable, `merge` keeps **both**, and `Dimension` declares whether the snapshot reading (collapse, correct) or the contested one applies. Clocks are **derived from the `causes` DAG the log already records**, so no field was added to `Event` and every R1 parity vector keeps passing untouched.

**Update 2026-08-17 — the OTHER two-clock split is closed (EVT-5, `clocks.rs`), and it is a different pair from EVT-8's.** EVT-8 split *physical time* from *causal time*; this splits **when it happened in the world** from **when the system heard about it**, and the gap between them is the skew. ★★ The load-bearing property is that a **negative** skew survives: `Skew::Observed` is signed, `skew_of` floors nothing, and `clock_findings` takes an **immutable** slice so the surfacing path cannot repair what it reports — the clamp §III warns about is unspellable, not merely discouraged. ★ Three readings, and two are not numbers: an absent arrival reads `Unknown` and a backfilled row reads `Inferred`, neither collapsing to a `0` that would pass for a punctual arrival. Skew is unbounded on purpose — there is no threshold and no error type anywhere in the module, so §III's week-long outage is accepted as it must be, and **EVT-6 is unblocked without being pre-empted**. ★ Two stale claims in this module's own rows were corrected while reconciling: `causes` was already present (EVT-1 listed it pending) and `provenance` was already present (EVT-5 listed it pending) — both were re-read rather than re-built. And one went the other way: `payload` was listed as present on the record and **is not**, so EVT-1 stays 🔨 with that residual named. **Update 2026-08-17 (same day, straight on) — EVT-6 closed, and it is the row the whole substrate was pointing at.** ★★ *No window closes by wall-clock hope* is now **structural**: closing takes a `&Watermark` and nothing else, so a caller holding only a processing time cannot ask. §4J.3's failure — windows closing because a date rolled over — is exactly `W(τ) = τ`, and it remains **possible but no longer accidental**: it requires declaring `max_out_of_orderness: 0`, the claim *this source never delivers out of order*, out loud. A conformance case runs the zero bound and an honest 2h bound at the same τ over the same window and shows one closing and the other not. ★ Monotonicity is structural and **explicitly distinguished from EVT-5's clamp rule** — there, flooring a measurement hides a fact; here, non-decreasing *is* the definition, and a retreat is reported rather than discarded. ★ The heuristic is **estimated from EVT-5's surfaced skew**, honouring its three readings (an `Unknown` or `Inferred` skew contributes nothing, and with none observed the estimator errors rather than returning a zero bound — *an unmeasured source is not a punctual one*), and **no volume of data ever promotes it to a guarantee**. ★ And because a heuristic will sometimes be wrong, the **lateness policy is mandatory** — `Lateness` has no `Default`, so the compiler enforces it — with each cost real in the type: only `AccumulateAndRetract` retains its emitted value, so `Drop` cannot retract even by mistake. ★ The divergence is a **specific failure rather than an absence**: the reference has three real windows (`calendar.upcoming`, `tasks.due_soon`, `protege.urgent_window_hours`) and every one closes on `ctx.timestamp` — with a genuine credit alongside it, that clock is **injected not ambient**, so EVT-13's purity holds and the defect is the value assumed, not an ambient read. **Update 2026-08-17 (third slice the same day) — EVT-11's mechanism closed, completing §VIII's *same object seen twice* in one place.** `Period` wraps EVT-6's `Window`, so the two halves meet rather than sitting beside each other. ★★ The partition is **structural** — each period's end *is* the next one's start — which buys Dijkstra's point where it matters: **a year is the sum of its twelve months, not the months plus twelve boundary instants**. ★★ **Store the rule, recompute the expansion** is a property of the type (`Recurrence` holds no instants), and the reason is *run*: one rule under two tzdata releases yields two different instants with an identical local time. ★★ **Zone id over bare offset**, with `FixedOffset` kept as a labelled lesser thing whose trap is demonstrated — it agrees today and diverges under the revised release. ★ The tz data is **injected** (`TzProvider`), and §VIII's own argument is the reason rather than the ADR alone: a bundled database pins a release invisibly. Civil-calendar arithmetic is computed (fixed algorithm); timezone offsets are only asked for (versioned data). **No new dependency.** ★ DST is exercised by a Berlin counterparty since Nairobi's case is vacuous — a real 23-hour and 25-hour day, both still partitioning. **Update 2026-08-17 (fourth slice the same day) — EVT-12 closed** (`checkpoint.rs`). ★★ Discardability and derived-never-authored are both **structural**: one function with an `Option` argument, and no constructor that takes a state. ★★ Replay **cannot reach an effect channel** — no sink, no registry, no `execute` — so §IX's assertion about external effects is a property of the signature. ★★ And it found a real gap in §IX's own pseudocode: the per-call `seen` set makes the checkpointed and from-scratch paths **disagree on a late duplicate**, demonstrated by running the pseudocode literally; the fix is taken at the substrate, where §V already puts dedupe. ★ The counterweight is generous and exact: the reference **already runs the homomorphism** (`sustain_states` advances one call at a time — `fold(s_k, L[k+1..n])` at `k = n−1`) and its discardability is real and **tested** there (`rebuild_state()` regenerates the cache and the law is asserted). The genuine difference is that `_persist_state()` makes a wrong cache *writable* and forbids it by docstring, where `Checkpoint::at` makes it unwritable. **Update 2026-08-17 (fifth slice the same day) — EVT-10 closed, and with it the module's centrepiece stops being remembered and starts being checked.** ★★ Delta-accumulation on a snapshot dimension is a **type error proven by a `compile_fail` doctest** that `cargo test --doc` executes — there is no `add` on `SnapshotDim`, so the mistake is unspellable — with a schema-level refusal for the half a type system cannot reach (a kind declared in a spec, an update off a wire). ★★ *Never accumulate deltas* is **derived and measured**: non-idempotence run as arithmetic, and the safe version's applied-id map at 200 entries against LWW's constant 1. ★★ §VII's emphatic limit is structural — three distinct kinds, `Contested` refusing automatic apply entirely and backed by the OR-set, so **LWW cannot eat a contested edit**. ★ And the counterweight is the strongest in the set: §VII's own field note says the LWW behaviour shipped in the reference **first**, derived empirically after out-of-order SMS clobbered a balance — *"the convergence theorem is not being proposed to VOS; it is being written down about VOS."* This row did not bring a behaviour; it turned a hard-won rule into a checked one. ★ `vclock::Dimension` was **extended, not forked**, which turned `resolve` into a typed `Resolved` outcome.

**★★ The Events & Time module's attack list is now complete** — EVT-5, EVT-6, EVT-11's mechanism, EVT-12 and EVT-10 all closed on 2026-08-17, on top of the convergence core that was already ✅. **Declared residuals, all named rather than quietly open:** EVT-11's **RRULE-grammar remainder** (`COUNT`/`UNTIL`/`BYSETPOS`/`YEARLY`/`BYMONTH`/`EXDATE`/…, an `RRULE:` text parser, and migrating a real consumer onto the primitive); EVT-10's **DSL authoring surface** (M-DSL, Phase 4) and its `State`/`StateAccessor` wiring; **EVT-14** effect journaling; and **EVT-1's `payload`** on the durable record.

**Update 2026-08-17 — EVT-15 closed, and it opens Phase 3.** ★★ §IX's **second** consumer, which EVT-12 deliberately left: the **semantic** layer, built *beside* the patch fold rather than into it. The premise was checked in the code first and held in all three parts — the patch fold is `D`-independent (**no parameter through which a definition could enter**), the logged call exists **in the reference's `operators_log` and not in the Rust `Event`** (so `EnzymeCall` had to be defined), and **`D′` needed no new type** because `editing::Definition` is already the gate's own three inputs. ★★ Under the same `D` the calls reproduce the patch state (asserted — without it every `D′` verdict would be about a different history); under a `D′` that bites, the history is **partly** inadmissible with the verdict on the specific call, and `NotPermitted` is kept separate from `Refused`. ★★ Replay emits nothing, guaranteed twice: structurally (no sink, no egress, no send in this core — **EVT-14 is not built in Rust**) and explicitly (`ReplayMode::Replay` discards **and counts**, so the guarantee survives an effect layer being added). ★ Tenet's consumer was already served by EVT-12 + the simulator; the new work is Editing's. **EDIT-11 is unblocked.**

### Layer 2 — DEFINITION

#### M-SUS · Sustain design (S)  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — Sustain - Formal Execution.md` (CELL). The one recursive object `Σ = ⟨B, S, V, T, ⊕⟩`. (Naming: Enzyme=operator, Symbiont=operative, Embroidery=dsl — canonical terms, code aliases until rename.)*

| # | Feature (from the article) | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| SUS-1 | **`Σ = ⟨B, S, V, T, ⊕⟩`** — the single recursive object; components of a Sustain are Sustains; no privileged top. Closes Phase 0 = ⟨S,T⟩ | I | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`sigma.rs`) — ★★★ **every component existed; the OBJECT did not.** `boundary.rs` is `B`, `schema.rs` is `S`, `region.rs` is `V`, `Definition` is `T`, `rollup.rs` composes — five real things assembled by whichever caller needed them, with nothing anywhere holding the claim that they are one thing. ★★★ *Components of a Sustain are Sustains* only becomes true when **one type says so**: two types, one for "a Sustain" and one for "a Sustain with children", would put a ceiling in the model the world does not have. A village adopts into a county by the same call a habitat joins a household. ★★★ **No privileged top is structural** — no `Root` type, no `is_root`; a root is one nobody has adopted yet, and a test asserts it is the *same value* before and after. ★★ A cycle is **unrepresentable** (children are owned), so the walk needs no visited-set that every other holon path in this codebase carries |
| SUS-2 | **S = typed state space** — product of named typed domains (record type, not JSON blob); path projection `π_p`; partial-state = projection onto subspace (enables modular/parallel eval) | II | ✅ (R2 #14 typed record state) |
| SUS-3 | **Type-checked predicates** — guard/invariant/postcondition referencing an undeclared dimension is a **load-time type error** (`bind(predicate_ast, schema)→errors`) | II | ✅ (predicate AST bound to schema at load) |
| SUS-4 | **Five required S properties** — serializable, addressable, composable, evolvable (schema versions without invalidating Enzymes), deterministic | II | ✅ **closed 2026-08-29 — verified, not flipped.** Serializable/addressable/composable were already ✅; **deterministic** is `canonical.rs` (SUS-15, 2026-08-29); **evolvable** is M-EDIT, which the phasing records complete every row — the append-only definition DAG (`version.rs`), `μ` with Expand–Migrate–Contract (`migrate.rs`), and the stranding check that refuses an edit which would leave a live instance outside `V` (`stranding.rs`). The row pointed at M-EDIT and M-EDIT closed; this records that rather than leaving a pointer to a finished module |
| SUS-5 | **State = fold** `s(t)=fold(events[0..t])`; monoid homomorphism/catamorphism; reproducibility, incrementality (cache==rebuild law), time-travel; ops replace_root/set/append/remove; `FoldError` never silent | III | ✅ (R1 fold; rebuild==get_state proven) |
| SUS-6 | **Patch-level vs semantic events** — patch replay can't diverge from what the Enzyme did; semantic layer addable later without migration (named debt) | III | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** — and the named debt was paid **without a migration**, exactly as the row predicted. ★★★ **The finding:** `semantic::replay_under` re-runs the logged *calls*, and the durable log recorded the operator and the resulting mutations but **not the params** — half a call, so the mechanism EDIT-11 stands on had nothing real to read. `LoggedEvent.params` is additive and `Option`, so every pre-slice line still deserialises byte-identically. ★★ `None` means *we do not know what it was called with*, which is genuinely different from *called with nothing*: `enzyme_calls` **skips and counts** those rather than replaying an empty map, because an Enzyme called with nothing is a different call and reporting on it would be reporting confidently on something that did not happen |
| SUS-7 | **B = boundary ⟨scope, μ⟩** — μ a **membership predicate** deciding what the Sustain owns; private-vs-shared is structural (where a var sits relative to B), not a permissions bolt-on | IV | ✅ **built 2026-08-17** (`boundary.rs` · `Boundary`, `BoundaryDecl`, `Scope`, `Owned`, `BoundaryChange`, `BoundaryToken`, `preserves_membership`, `admit_boundary_change`) — **Phase 1's keystone.** `B = ⟨scope, μ⟩` is a real membership predicate over `Entities ∪ Paths`, replacing exactly the model §IV names by its symptoms: the reference's `{"owner_ids": ["system"]}` is *"a state dict with an owner field"*. ★ **`owns` keeps §IV's TWO conjuncts as two** — `scope.contains(x) ∧ μ(x)` — so a path μ claims but scope does not span is **not owned**, which catches a Sustain declaring ownership of something it has no business in. ★ **μ is subscripted by the state, which is what makes the law checkable:** the roster is state, so who belongs is something an operator could move; `BoundaryDecl::read` evaluates μ AT a state and `μ_{o(s)} = μ_s` compares two real values. ★ **μ has two halves with different natures, and saying so is the accurate thing:** the path half is DECLARED (changing it is a definition edit — that higher gate already exists in `editing.rs`), the entity half lives in STATE and is the half an ordinary Enzyme could move, so that is where the closure law bites. An **unreadable** register is distinguished from an **empty** one — *"nobody is a member"* and *"the roster is not where it was declared"* are different facts, and an operator that deletes the roster is refused under its own reason rather than silently emptying the boundary. ★ **Person-first nesting is a checkable property, not a convention:** `owns_interior_of` is always false — a parent owns the child and **not** the paths inside it, which is the whole of *"composes into Homestead and Vyyb boundaries without dissolving"* |
| SUS-8 | **Autopoietic closure law** — `∀o∈T,∀s: μ_{o(s)}=μ_s ∧ schema(o(s))=schema(s)`; Enzymes change values inside B, never B itself; changing B is a distinct **higher gate** (add member, adopt child) | IV | ✅ **completed 2026-08-17** — the schema half shipped with R2 #14 and the **μ half joins it**: `preserves_closure` is the whole conjunction `μ_{o(s)} = μ_s ∧ schema(o(s)) = schema(s)`, enforced in `operator::execute` before the invariants. ★★ **Structural at the gate, not a convention:** a registered operator that appends to the roster is **refused, state untouched, no mutations, no events**, tagged `boundary_closure` — proven with a real operator through `execute`, not a predicate call. The two halves are **tagged apart** (`boundary_closure` vs `organisational_closure`) because they fail for different reasons: one wants a migration, the other a `BoundaryToken`. ★ **The separate boundary-change gate is built and REUSES the definition gate's authority** — `BoundaryToken::mint` takes the same `Governance` and `EditAuthority`, so a shared Sustain's boundary needs a `CouncilMint` and no single actor converts into one. **Two paths mean nothing to exempt**: an ordinary operator cannot reach μ at all, where a single-path design would have to carve the boundary operators out of its own law |
| SUS-9 | **V = viable region** — box of per-dim intervals ∩ cross-dimension constraints; **a region, not a boolean list** → measurable / projectable / intersectable / searchable (VI–VIII need this); `in_region(s,V)` | V | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (`region.rs`, 2026-08-12) — **V is a first-class region**: `Region { intervals, relations, weights }`, with all four operations the article says a list cannot do. **Measured** (`distance` → `W`), **projected** (`Distance::binding` names which dimension binds), **intersected** (`fits_within_report` — child ⊆ parent, with the offending dimension named), **searched** ⬜ (needs the kernel, SUS-10). Membership is **three-valued** (`Interior` / `Boundary` / `Outside`), because `W ≡ 0` inside cannot rank anything |
| SUS-10 | **Viab_T(V) = viability kernel** — states from which *some* legal-move sequence keeps s∈V **forever**; greatest fixed point by descending iteration `D_{k+1}=Φ(D_k)`; the only honest target for "keep alive"; limits: exponential in dims (needs sampled/receding-horizon approx), finite-horizon `Viab^H` is an optimistic superset | VI | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (`kernel.rs`, 2026-08-12) — the greatest fixed point by **descending iteration** `D₀=V, D_{k+1}=Φ(D_k)`, terminating on an enumerated/discretised space. **Both of §VI's honest limitations are ENCODED, not worked around:** (1) exponential-in-dimensions → the exact computation runs over an **explicitly enumerated** space and claims nothing about states nobody enumerated; (2) `Viab^H` is a **superset** and therefore **optimistic** → `Kernel::Horizon` is a separate variant with **no constructor that turns it into `Exact`**, so reporting an optimistic set as a guarantee is *unspellable*. A vector proves `Viab^H ⊇ Viab_T(V)` at several horizons. **The headline: a state in `V` and outside the kernel — *fine right now, already lost*** |
| SUS-11 | **Lyapunov control** — `W(s)=d(s,V)`; pick Enzyme minimizing `W(o(s))`; descent ≥α ⇒ re-enter in ⌈W/α⌉ steps; weights `w` declared (not buried); inside V secondary objective = **kernel margin**; rollback = declared inverse to lower-W state | VII | ✅ **complete 2026-08-29** — `W(s)=d(s,V)` ✅ and the kernel margin ✅ (2026-08-12); **picking the Enzyme that minimises `W(o(s))` ✅ and the `⌈W/α⌉` step bound ✅** (2026-08-29, `control_system.rs`). ★★★ The bound is what turns *this is improving* into *this ends*: a loop closing half the remaining gap every turn descends forever, arrives never, and passes the stability check at every single step. `Unbounded` is named rather than reported as a very large number — "never, at this rate" says *look for a bigger move*, where a big number reads as patience being enough. *(Original note:)* (2026-08-12) — the weighted distance with `W(s)=0 ⟺ s∈V̄`, declared weights `w` (`undeclared_weights()` surfaces every silent `1.0`), and **`kernel_margin()` now supersedes the box margin**: `region.rs`'s `margin()` measures the edge of `V` and was labelled an honest **over**-estimate when it shipped; the kernel edge is nearer, and a vector measures both (20 → 10). **Still ⬜: the argmin-over-Enzymes control step and the α-descent guarantee** — those need `T` and belong with M-CTL/M-TEN |
| SUS-12 | **⊕ composition + ρ roll-up** — `S_P = own × ∏children × A`; aggregate `⊗_a` must be a **commutative monoid** (sum/min/max/count ok; avg → carry (sum,count), divide at end); computed fresh, never persisted | VIII | ✅ (Slice 7 + R2 composition/rollup) |
| SUS-13 | **Roll-up rules** — ρ is a function not a variable (no stale total, no reconciliation); **missing ≠ zero** (excluded + named); **nested viability** (whole viable ⟺ every level viable); **holon law** — parent value is a *fold over* members, never an *overwrite* (structural via monoid) | VIII | ✅ (excluded+named, is_self parent-fold, holon-by-construction) |
| SUS-14 | **Per-rule authority, advisory by default** — binding aggregate invariant refuses a child transition only on a **newly-caused** breach (person-first: already-breached parent doesn't block a non-worsening child action) | VIII | ✅ (R2 `_check_parent_binding_gate`, authority default advisory) |
| SUS-15 | **Determinism + canonical hash** — Enzymes pure (non-determinism captured into the event); `H(s)=hash(canonical(s))` makes verification a comparison; `fold(log)==cache` fails loudly on divergence | IX | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`canonical.rs`) — purity ✅ structural, and `H(s)` now real. ★★★ Sorting object keys is not tidiness: this crate builds its `Map` with `preserve_order`, so hashing insertion order would report a household as diverged **from itself**. Numbers go through the engine's own `num` rule for the same reason — a cache written before it and a fold replayed after it would otherwise hash differently while being the same household. Array order is preserved, because in a list order IS the value. ★★ Adds `sha2`, named rather than slipped in: `H(s)` is compared across peers that need not trust each other, and a hash somebody can collide on purpose verifies nothing. Wired to the host as `sustain_hash`, over the folded log with no cache to drift from |
| SUS-16 | **Domain declaration (Cynefin)** — `domain: R→{clear,complicated,complex,chaotic}` **per region** of S; disorder = failure-mode to detect/surface; domain is a *reading* (Monitor detects shift via CUSUM); justified by Ashby requisite variety + Conant-Ashby "good regulator = model" | X | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`domain_map.rs`) — `cynefin.rs` had the taxonomy and nothing that could produce a reading; its own doc said inventing a classifier would be *a confident classification from nowhere*, so the household declares it, per region. ★★★ **A state nothing covers is DISORDER, never `clear`** — defaulting to clear licenses best practice, so an unrecognised situation would be met with the method that presupposes it is already understood. ★★★ **Two declarations that disagree are also disorder**, not a precedence puzzle: picking the first would hide a real contradiction behind a confident answer. Two that AGREE are not a conflict, so a household's regions need not be a perfect partition |
| SUS-17 | **Goodhart guard** — for every un-optimised dimension `d∉U` (U=∪ Symbiont utility dims), auto-emit a bound invariant into V so un-scored dims are gated structurally, not by review habit | XI | ✅ **complete 2026-08-29** (`unscored.rs`) — ★★★ **the auto-emit half, built without inventing a single number.** Its own recorded reason for not building it stands and is honoured: a fabricated threshold over a dimension nobody watches is a confident number from nowhere. But there IS a bound that is not invented — **the dimension's own declared type**. `Number { lo: 0 }` is the household saying, in the definition, that it does not go below zero, and emitting an invariant from that converts a bound the schema *describes* into one the gate *holds*. Everything else is **named, not bounded**: unscored and unbounded dimensions are listed so a person can see what nothing is watching and nothing is holding. An open map is left to a quantifier rather than enumerated — rules over today's keys would silently fail to cover tomorrow's pocket, which is the worst kind because the list looks complete. *(Original note:)* 🔨 **half built 2026-08-16** (= OPV-27, `goodhart.rs`) — `U` is computable, a guard over it is declarable, and the **gate enforces it**; a guard that watches nothing unwatched is refused. ★ **The AUTO-EMIT half is deliberately NOT built**, and the reason is a real one rather than scheduling: auto-emitting a *bound* invariant requires knowing **what bound**, and nobody declared one. A fabricated threshold over a dimension nobody was watching would be exactly the confident-number-from-nowhere this codebase refuses elsewhere (`0/0 ≠ r = 0`; a missing cost is not free; a missing reading is not zero). What could be auto-emitted honestly is the **finding** — *"this dimension is unwatched and has no guard"* — which is what `Coverage` already reports. Emitting a real bound needs a person to say what it is |

**Summary:** the core — typed S, fold, load-time predicate binding, composition/roll-up with the holon law + person-first authority — is ✅ in Rust. **Not started:** the viability kernel `Viab_T(V)`, Lyapunov homeostatic control (shared with Controller), the Cynefin domain field, the Goodhart guard, canonical-hash verification, and the full μ membership boundary. **★ Update 2026-08-17 — μ IS NOW BUILT, and it is Phase 1's keystone** (`boundary.rs`, SUS-7/SUS-8/OP-10): `B = ⟨scope, μ⟩` replaces the flat `owner_ids` §IV names by its symptoms, the **autopoietic closure law is enforced in both halves at the gate** (`μ_{o(s)} = μ_s ∧ schema(o(s)) = schema(s)`), and boundary change is a **separate higher gate reusing `editing.rs`'s authority** rather than a second model. Two paths mean **nothing to exempt** — an ordinary operator cannot reach μ at all. This **unblocks the firewall `F`** (CON-1), which was blocked on exactly this and is the immediate next slice. Region-as-first-class-object and schema-versioning (evolvable) are partial (→ M-CON, M-EDIT). **Update 2026-08-12:** **V is now a first-class region in Rust** (`region.rs`) — measurable, projectable and intersectable, with `W(s) = d(s,V)` and declared weights. That closes SUS-9 and the distance half of SUS-11, and it is the substrate SUS-10's viability kernel now has something to be a subset *of*. **Update 2026-08-12 (second):** **`Viab_T(V)` now exists** (`kernel.rs`) — the descending iteration to the greatest fixed point, with both of §VI's limitations encoded in the types rather than footnoted, plus **runway `R(s)`**, the cheap forward-scan clock the Operative article names as the best value per line of code once `V` is a region. Together these close SUS-10 and OPV-19, upgrade SUS-11's margin from the box to the kernel edge, and sharpen CTL-2. **This is the ingredient Tenet's backward induction needed.**

#### M-OP · Operator (T)  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — Operator - Formal Execution.md` (ENZYME). The guarded transition; the whole registry IS T. Guard→effect→emit.*

| # | Feature | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| OP-1 | **Enzyme = ⟨guard g, effect e, emission ε⟩** — partial map `dom(o)={(s,p):g_o}`; partiality is where the law lives; **every effect must emit** (keeps state=fold honest) | I | ✅ (R1 operators; emit mandatory) |
| OP-2 | **Hoare triple `{g} e {Q}`** — pre/postcondition; refusal computed on a **copy**, judged, *then* committed (nothing durable until Q passes) | II | ✅ (R2 gate checks post on candidate) |
| OP-3 | **Weakest-precondition obligation** `g ⟹ wp(e,Q)` — static author-time claim the guard is strong enough; flag an Enzyme whose Q its guard can't guarantee | II | 🔨 **`wp` itself is built** (`compose.rs`, 2026-08-12) — the pullback stays inside the predicate fragment for an unchanged path, a `SetTo` (constant-folds to always/never) and a `ShiftBy` (moves the literal). **✅ COMPLETED 2026-08-17** (`obligation.rs` · `Soundness`, `ObligationReport`, `check_obligation`, `audit`, `flagged`; `OperatorMeta.effect`). The `EffectSummary` is **`Option`, absent by default** — no operator declaration was force-touched and no behaviour changed. ★★ **It FLAGS, it does not BLOCK**, which is the posture §III actually takes (*'should at least be flagged, not silently trusted'*): nothing here is called from `execute`, the per-call dynamic check is untouched, and a test asserts an unsound operator is **flagged at author time AND still runs**, caught dynamically after the fact — *'both are useful, and they are not the same thing.'* ★ **Three honest outcomes, and the third is the point:** `Sound`, `Unsound` (the flag), and **`Unavailable`** — because both other readings of *we cannot tell* are wrong. Silently passing an unsummarisable effect is exactly the *silently trusted* this row exists to prevent; flagging one would report a defect that has not been shown. **An unknown effect is not a proven-wrong one.** ★★ **What running it over the real registry FOUND:** every shipped operator is `Unavailable`, because Sustena's effects are **parameter-driven** — the amount comes from the call, which the fragment itself names `Opaque`. So the static obligation's reach today is **zero operators proven either way**, which is precisely why §III says *'the honest position for Sustena today is the dynamic one'* — the check does not contradict that sentence, **running it is what demonstrates it**. Asserted in a test, so a future summarisable operator breaks it and forces a look. Reuses `compose.rs`'s `wp` and `entails` unchanged; builds no prover. Limit recorded: **a summary is a declaration and can be wrong** — checking it against the body is a separate, larger row, which is why it is absent by default rather than inferred |
| OP-4 | **Determinism spectrum** pure/bounded/impure; **capture every non-deterministic input into the event** so replay sees recorded not fresh | III | ✅ structural — Rust core pure; identity+time from host (captured) |
| OP-5 | **Checked composition** `post(A) ⊨ guard(B)` at authoring time; composite guard `g_A ∧ wp(e_A,g_B)`; 3-valued **entailed/refuted/undecided**; decidable on the Embroidery fragment or via SMT | IV | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (`compose.rs`, 2026-08-12) — `compose(A,B)` returns `Result<Composed, Rejected>`, so a **refuted chain yields no composite value at all**, and `Pathway::try_chain` has **no constructor that takes steps on trust** — *a recipe whose third step can never fire cannot be held*, not merely rejected on save. All three parts derived: `g_A ∧ wp(e_A,g_B)`, `post(B)`, `ε_A · ε_B` (cost additive). **Associativity is checked, not assumed.** ⊨ is decided over conjunctions of path-vs-literal **interval** constraints; everything else returns **undecided** with `runtime_guard_required` + a reason — never a false claim. No SMT solver pulled in (a dependency-free core is what ships to a phone) |
| OP-6 | **Declared inverses** — `e_{o⁻¹}(e_o(s))=s` + `image(e_o)⊆dom(o⁻¹)`; reversible subset `T_r`; **generic patch-level inverse** from recorded `{op,path,old,new}`; ties to Controller (exact W restoration) | V | ✅ (R2 #15 patch-level inverse from the record) |
| OP-7 | **Effective guard** `ĝ = g_o ∧ ⋀ invariants(e_o(s)) ∧ permitted(actor)` — three distinct refusals: **not now / not ever / not you** (never collapse to "denied") | VI | ✅ — all three terms in Rust (guard ✅, invariants-of-result ✅, privilege ✅ R2 #10) |
| OP-8 | **Reference monitor** — complete mediation (every access checked, unbypassable), fail-safe defaults, least privilege; `e_o(s)∈V` in every gate ⇒ **V is an induction invariant** | VI | ✅ **structural win** — Rust core has one execution path, so complete mediation holds by construction (Python's was opt-in/bypassable) |
| OP-9 | **T = the transition model** `⟨S,T⟩` labelled transition system; reachability/viability/control/simulation/planning all defined over T | VII | ✅ |
| OP-10 | **Closure + completeness** — Enzymes preserve boundary+schema (autopoiesis); any mutation without an event is **forced into the log** (`event.system.unlogged_state_change`) | VII | ✅ **complete 2026-08-17** — structural (one write path), and the **boundary-change higher-gate now exists** (= SUS-7/SUS-8, `boundary::BoundaryToken`). Autopoietic closure is enforced in both halves at `operator::execute`, so *Enzymes preserve boundary + schema* is a refusal rather than a description |
| OP-11 | **Price of a move** — declared `cost(o)` (gas-style, up front) vs **metered ⟨compute,storage⟩** (odometer, after); **compute = work not wall-clock** (reproducible); guard is metered; refused = zero | VIII | ⏸️ economy-deferred (Python meter exists; Rust metering rides the economy layer) |

**Summary:** the Enzyme core is ✅ in Rust, and the effective guard is **more complete than Python** — Rust makes complete mediation *structural* (one path) and enforces privilege (the "not you" term). **Checked composition shipped 2026-08-12** (`compose.rs`, R2 #16): illegal chains are now discovered at WRITE time rather than by running them and watching step three refuse, and a refuted pathway is unconstructible rather than merely refused. **Update 2026-08-17: OP-3 is ✅** (`obligation.rs`) — the author-time obligation `g ⟹ wp(e,Q)` now runs on a single Enzyme, off the execution path, as the **flag** §III asks for rather than a gate term. Its finding is worth carrying: **every shipped operator reports `Unavailable`**, because Sustena's effects are parameter-driven and the fragment names that `Opaque` — which is exactly why §III calls the dynamic check the honest position today. **With OP-3 closed and the boundary/firewall rows before it, the Operator layer has no open rows left but metering** (economy-deferred). Python's audited gaps (opt-in gate, unread privilege, no inverse) are exactly what Rust closed by construction.

#### M-CON · Constraint engine (V binding)  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — Constraint - Formal Execution.md` (LAW). The gate that makes V binding; the induction that keeps V true. (Tolerance/self–non-self layer is M-IMM.)*

| # | Feature | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| CON-1 | **Three predicate families** — **state** `C:S→bool` (A=∩C_i=V), **transition** `D:S×S→bool` (over the pair), **boundary/firewall** `F(φ,s)` (flow across B); none reduces to another; composition = **conjunction only** (monotone, no priority/override) | I | ✅ **state `C` ✅ and transition `D` ✅** (`transition.rs`, 2026-08-12) — `D` is the gate's third conjunct, evaluated over the (before, after) **pair**, with all three canonical shapes: **rate limit**, **monotonicity** and **conservation**. Proven strictly more expressive by a vector where the after-state is *individually valid* and only the STEP is refused. **Firewall `F` ✅ built 2026-08-17** (`flow.rs` · `Movement`, `Flow`, `Crossing`, `FlowRule`, `classify_movement`, `flows_of`, `check_flows`) — **all three families now ship**, and `F` got its own module for the same reason `D` did: `C` is over a state, `D` is over a pair, `F` is over **neither**. ★★ **Irreducibility PROVEN, not asserted:** 500 moved between two own pockets versus 500 out to a casino and 500 back produce **byte-identical** before and after states, and the case runs the SAME `Conservation` rule with `Tolerance::Exact` — `D`'s strongest form — over both and asserts it **passes both**. Only `F` separates them. ★ **A flow is defined relative to `μ`, not independently:** an operator declares a `Movement` (what moved, between whom) and `μ` decides whether it crossed `B` and which way — pocket-to-pocket is internal **because μ owns both ends**, not because the operator said so. Neither end owned is reported as `Foreign`, never silently dropped. ★ **Flows are DECLARED, never inferred:** the effect returns them (§II's `candidate, flows = o.effect(...)`), because if they were recoverable by diffing endpoints `F` would be reducible to `D`. Four declared rule shapes, no `eval`; `OnlyKnown` uses `F(φ,s)`'s **state** argument so a rule can be about the household's own contacts rather than a static allowlist. ★ **Conjunction only** — a more permissive rule cannot undo a stricter one, asserted directly. ★ **The clamp trap honoured by CONSTRUCTION:** `FlowRule` has **no clamp strategy at all**, so capping one side of a crossing — §III's money-minting move — is unrepresentable rather than refused. Wired into the gate as `admit_F`'s fourth conjunct; the household's real case is proven at `execute`, where the same operator and amount is admitted from a known counterparty and refused from a stranger, state byte-identical Composition is conjunction only, so adding a rule can only shrink the admissible region |
| CON-2 | **The gate** `admit(o,s) ⟺ g_o(s) ∧ o(s)∈A ∧ D(s,o(s))` (+ `⋀F(φ,s)`); effect on a copy → judge candidate → commit only if admitted | II | ✅ **all three conjuncts now built** — `g_o(s)` ✅, `o(s)∈A` ✅, **`D(s,o(s))` ✅ (2026-08-12)**, and **`⋀F(φ,s)` ✅ (2026-08-17)** — `admit_F(o,s) = admit(o,s) ∧ ⋀_{φ∈flows} F(φ,s)` is now the whole gate, all four conjuncts built. The effect returns `(candidate, flows)` exactly as §II's pseudocode writes it. Effect on a copy → judge the candidate → commit only if admitted, unchanged |
| CON-3 | **The induction** — `s_0∈A` + every change admitted ⟹ `s_k∈A ∀k`; **one skip deletes the theorem** → complete mediation mandatory | III | ✅ **structural** (Rust: one write path) |
| CON-4 | **Safety vs liveness** (Schneider) — a monitor enforces **exactly safety, never liveness**; "stays in V" ✅, "a move always exists" ✗ → needs the viability kernel (CON-10) | III | ✅ **DONE** — kernel built (`core/kernel.rs`) and now admitted against (CON-10). |
| CON-5 | **Complete mediation / reference monitor** — always invoked, tamper-proof, small; **fail-safe defaults** (a constraint that fails to compile must NOT silently pass); economy of mechanism; least privilege | IV | ✅ — **Rust closed fail-open** (R2 #8: unreadable rule refuses) + one path |
| CON-6 | **Three strategies: refuse / clamp / defer** — clamp = projection `Π_A(s')` (only convex/interval constraints; **never clamp a conserved quantity**; report asked+did); defer = proposal, **re-admitted at commit** (verdict 4-valued) | V | ✅ **Rust win** (R2 #11 + 3 clamp safety rules; Python had refuse only) |
| CON-7 | **Conservation as a transition constraint** — `|SUM(after[q])−SUM(before[q])| ≤ tolerance`, refuse; money = integer minor units, tolerance 0; "shadow of a sameness," not a target | VI | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (2026-08-12) — **money is conserved is expressible for the first time**. `Tolerance::Exact` sums as `i128` over whole minor units, so a float cannot enter the arithmetic and *tolerance 0 is genuinely exact*; a **fractional** value is refused, never truncated (an integral float like `95000.0` is accepted — same count, differently written, and the engine's arithmetic promotes). **`TransitionRule` carries no strategy field at all**, so *never clamp a conserved quantity* is not a check that could be forgotten — the clamp is unspellable |
| CON-8 | **Holon-path composition** `admissible_global = ⋀_{H∈path} admit_H` along the membership DAG; monotone, order-independent, name-the-refuser; advisory-by-default, binding on **newly-caused** breach | VII | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`admissibility.rs`) — the **full path**, not the nearest edge. Only the immediate parent was ever asked, so a rule two edges up simply did not apply, silently. Monotone and order-independent are executed as tests rather than asserted; every level is reported rather than stopping at the first refusal (a person told about one broken rule, who fixes it and is then told about a second, has been made to do the work twice). ★★★ **Finding:** an unreadable rule evaluates false both before AND after, so it looked exactly like a pre-existing breach and was waved through as *not newly caused* — the gate failing open right after somebody mistyped a law. `Reading` is three-valued now: held / failed / **unreadable**, and DSL-5's *broken document is not violated law* arrives here too |
