| CON-9 | **Total, impoverished evaluator** — every predicate terminates, side-effect-free, deterministic (no `eval()`); static undecidable (Rice) → 3-valued entailed/refuted/undecided; **aggregates SUM/COUNT/AVG/MIN/MAX** over wildcard = same as roll-up ρ; scope-indexing + compiled caching | IX | ✅ **Rust unified the two evaluators** (R2 #4, aggregates on both paths); scope-indexing ⬜. **Per the Note's correction:** in Python the gap is *split* — `predicates.py` (invariant/gate path) implements all five aggregates; `constraints.py` (Enzyme **guard** path) still returns *"unsupported aggregate/block expression"*. **The divergence IS the finding**, and Rust closing it is the fix |
| CON-9b | **⚠ "Aggregate" carries TWO distinct meanings and they must not be conflated** — **(a)** the `SUM`/`COUNT`/`AVG` **functions** in the predicate fragment (CON-9); **(b)** `_compile_spec_invariants`'s **`is_aggregate` flag**, marking an invariant that references a **roll-up dimension** declared in `spec["aggregates"]` — a value folded from linked children, *never part of this sustain's own state*. Sense-(b) invariants are `enforced: False` in the sustain's own gate **by design**, and instead govern children through `_check_parent_binding_gate` | Note | ✅ both senses implemented correctly in Rust; recorded because conflating them silently breaks CON-8 |
| CON-10 | **Strong form** — substitute `Viab_T(V)` for A so admittance implies a surviving next move (safety→liveness-content); expensive, depends on honesty of T, horizon-limited optimistic | VIII | ✅ **DONE** — `core/strong_admit.rs`, 9 tests. `admit_strong` returns five verdicts, not a bool: `Survivable` (exact kernel, the only guarantee), `ProvisionallySurvivable{horizon}` (a horizon kernel is a **superset** of the true one, so it admits and says so rather than promising), `Doomed` (inside `V`, no legal sequence keeps it there — the case the weak gate cannot see), `Outside`, `Unknown`. **An empty kernel admits**: no state surviving says the model is wrong more often than the household is doomed, and refusing on it would freeze somebody's money over an analysis nobody checked. `doomed_but_viable()` makes what the row buys countable — if it is empty on a household, the cost is not worth paying, which is a real answer. (= SUS-10) |

**Summary:** the gate is ✅ and **better than Python in three ways** — refuse/clamp/defer, one unified evaluator with aggregates, and no fail-open, all structural in Rust. **Real gaps:** **transition constraints D** (rate-limit, monotonicity, **conservation of money — currently inexpressible**), ~~**firewall constraints F**~~ — **✅ built 2026-08-17** (`flow.rs`): all three predicate families now ship, with `F`'s irreducibility to `C` and `D` **proven** — identical endpoints, both conserving under `Tolerance::Exact`, and only `F` separates them, the **full holon-path DAG**, and the **strong viability-kernel form**.

#### M-DSL · DSL (Embroidery / Genome)  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — DSL - Formal Execution.md` (GENOME). The one language a Sustain is written in; a definition **denotes** ⟨S,T,V⟩ — compile or refuse, never partial.*

| # | Feature | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DSL-1 | **Compiler pipeline** text→lex→parse→AST→resolve→type-check→lower→⟨S,T,V⟩; `C: Text⇀Σ` = `Ok(Σ)` or `Err({ε})`, **never a partial Sustain** | I | ✅ **complete 2026-08-29** — the type-check half landed with DSL-5 and is wired into `editing::typecheck`, so a definition either compiles or is refused with the mistake named. The three findings stay distinguishable: cannot READ it (parse), cannot RESOLVE it (bind), cannot TYPE it (`Γ⊢e:τ`) |
| DSL-2 | **Denotational semantics** — `⟦state_schema⟧=S`, `⟦invariant⟧=set`, `⟦operator⟧=⟨g,e,ε⟩`; **compositional** (no hidden control flow → licenses gate short-circuit/scope-index/candidate-eval); a definition is a **value**; the real argument against `eval()` | II | ✅ (immutable AST, no eval) |
| DSL-3 | **Grammar** — context-free BNF; precedence = grammar depth; ~LL(1); **small = a commitment** (buys totality, decidable entailment, economy, computable MDL) | III | ✅ (ported predicate grammar) |
| DSL-4 | **Name resolution** `Γ⊢p:τ` (wildcard resolves through a container); quantifier scoping `Γ⊕fields(τ_item)` right-biased; resolver + evaluator must agree; **undeclared path = load-time error naming the path** | IV | ✅ (bound to schema at load) |
| DSL-5 | **Type judgments `Γ⊢e:τ`** — comparison requires **same type + ordered**; "well-typed programs can't go wrong"; **the gate has no 4th outcome for "rule broken"** → without typing, a mistyped rule arrives as a *refusal* (can't tell a violated law from a broken document) | V | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`predicate/types.rs`) — ordering needs the same type, **equality is total** (`a == b` across two types is honestly false; refusing it would outlaw "is this empty"), `Any` and an undeclared param are **compatible with everything** because unstated is not the same as wrong, and `SUM` over text is caught at author time. ★★ Reuses `Schema::resolve` rather than growing a second path-walker free to drift from the binder and the evaluator |
| DSL-6 | **★ Exactly ONE language** — two evaluators = the language stops denoting (`SUM` = a sum in an invariant, an error in a guard); Deutsch discrete-alphabet universality; Landin many-surfaces-one-core | X | ✅ **Rust win** (R2 #4 unified the evaluators — Python had 2 implementations + 3 syntaxes) |
| DSL-7 | **Lowering** `lower: Def→⟨S,T,V,s0,operatives⟩`; **genesis event** (seeded state emitted AS an event → `state=fold` from instant 0); total on well-typed input | VI | ✅ (instantiate + genesis) |
| DSL-8 | **Parameterized `Def(θ)`** — typed function into Sustains, **checked once**, instantiated freely (substitution lemma); vs textual `{{token}}` (no theorem; survives as literal) | VII | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`parameter.rs`) — declared parameters with types and defaults; **checked once** and the substitution lemma asserted as a property (one check, many θ, each result conforming without re-checking). ★★★ **The `role_in_family` bug is caught at AUTHOR time** — the mistake was never in anybody's argument, it was a template referring to something it never declared. ★★ A whole-value `"{{limit}}"` keeps the parameter's own TYPE; an embedded one interpolates |
| DSL-9 | **Typed overlay `ρ`** — well-typed iff every path is a declared dim of that type; overlays compose (monoid); **lets you hand a person the *overlay*, never the definition** (Curated UI safety) | VIII | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`overlay.rs`) — right-biased union, monoid laws executed (associativity is what lets a surface accumulate a field at a time). ★★★ **Finding: per-path typing is NOT enough.** Every path can be a declared dim of the right type and the RESULT still malformed — setting one field of a new open-map key invents half a pocket, so the shape changes anyway, one key at a time. `apply` validates the whole candidate; the per-path judgment stays because it gives the better message when it is the one that fails |
| DSL-10 | **Holon path type-checked as one program** — a parent's binding rule must type-check against child dims + declared roll-up dims; catches an aggregate over a missing dim / summing a string, at author time | IX | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`holon_typing.rs`) — `Γ_parent ⊕ {aggregate_id : Number}`, checked against **every** child rather than a sample (a holon of habitats and a shop is ordinary). ★★★ Both failures it catches are **silent**: an aggregate over a dimension no child declares totals zero contributions, and summing a string skips every child — each reads exactly like a household that genuinely has nothing. `COUNT` is exempted deliberately: how many children have a label is a real question, what their labels sum to is not |
| DSL-11 | **Totality + decidability** — no recursion/unbounded loop/user-function; quantifiers **bounded over declared finite containers**; type-check decidable; "does o preserve c" undecidable (Rice) → 3-valued | X | ✅ structural (total evaluator) |
| DSL-12 | **Whole-spec validator** (author-time) — bind Enzyme refs, Symbiont names, aggregate declarations, `ui_schema` widget names, `access_policy` at load; `imports` + enforced versioning declared, unbuilt | VI | 🔨 **mostly done 2026-08-29** (`spec.rs`) — Enzyme refs bound against the real registry, duplicate rule/aggregate ids reported as **findings not tie-breaks**, an aggregate id shadowing a real dimension refused, and the holon path folded in as one door. Wired into the host's author-time check ahead of the type pass (names first, then types). ★★★ It closes the same class DSL-5 did: a spec naming an Enzyme nothing provides used to load, and the first person to try it was told the operator *is not available on this sustain* — a permission-shaped message for a typo. **Residual: `imports` + enforced versioning** (no `imports` mechanism exists yet), and widget-name binding, which `WidgetSet::load` already performs at its own door |
| DSL-13 | **Enzymes authored in Embroidery** — guards + effects declared in the DSL, not code (today a spec declares *which* Enzymes, never their guard/effect — `⟦operator⟧` is a name lookup) | §4F | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`embroidery.rs`) — `⟨g, e, ε, μ⟩` as data: guard predicates in the same language invariants use, actions (set/increment/decrement) over declared paths, declared emissions and declared movements. **Runs inside the gate** via one dispatch at the single existing call site, so it faces the same admission, the same invariants and the same double-entry check. ★★★ **Its effect summary is DERIVED from the actions, so it cannot lie** — `obligation` says a native summary is a claim that may be wrong; here the claim and the thing are one object. ★★ No arithmetic in expressions (the arithmetic is in the actions, exactly as `StateAccessor` already works) and no control flow — sequencing belongs to `dag.rs` where it is checked |
| DSL-14 | **Friendlier surface syntax** — many surfaces over one AST (safe only once DSL-6 holds); JSON is the only surface today | X | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`surface.rs`) — a line-oriented text surface for an authored Enzyme, parsing to the **same** `AuthoredEnzyme` the builder produces (asserted with `==`, which is DSL-6 cashed in). ★★★ The guard is handed to the ONE predicate parser untouched — a second one here is the two-evaluator failure DSL-6 names, and it would arrive silently. `parse(render(e)) == e` is executed, which is what makes it a surface rather than an importer. ★★ Every error carries a real line number; "invalid syntax" for a forty-line document makes somebody re-read all forty. ★★ Found and fixed on the way: `ParamDecl::name` was `&'static str`, so a run-time-authored parameter could only be had by **leaking** it — a defect that grows with use rather than one a test sees. Now `Cow` |
| DSL-15 | **Description-length / lift proposal (MDL)** — count how often a pattern recurs across Genomes; `ΔL(π)=n(L(body)−L(call))−L(def)`; **lift when ΔL>0** (over typed ASTs, human-confirmed, owes the migration predicate); learning = compression (same op as Symbiont memes) | XI | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`lift.rs`) — `ΔL` computed over the **typed AST**, never the text, so two rules differing in whitespace are one rule and the formatter is not part of the arithmetic. ★★★ `n` is the whole formula: a pattern used twice usually does not pay for the definition it would need, and the numbers say so rather than taste. **Proposals only** — `ΔL>0` says a lift would be shorter, not that the household wants it or that six pockets rhyming this month is a concept; a person and the migration predicate stand between it and the definition, the same division `learned.rs` keeps for parse rules |

**Summary (updated 2026-08-29 — every row closed):** the front end (parse, resolve, lower, one unified evaluator) is ✅ — and Rust's single evaluator satisfies the article's hard "exactly one language" rule (DSL-6, a Python gap closed). **Key gaps:** the **type-checker `Γ⊢e:τ`** (DSL-5), the **typed overlay** (DSL-9), the whole-spec validator, holon-path joint typing, **Enzymes authored in the DSL** (DSL-13), and the MDL lift-proposal loop.

#### M-IMM · Immune / security (TOLERANCE)  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — Immune - Formal Execution.md` (TOLERANCE). Security is **not a module** — it's a property of B and the gate. Mediation, authority, detection across the recursive Sustain.*

**Key result: Sustena is "architecturally correct, evaluationally incomplete" — and Rust closes most of the evaluation gaps structurally. Article's build order: gate unconditional + fail-closed → principal term → grant-as-transfer → sign catalogue → detection LAST.**

| # | Feature | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| IMM-1 | **Full admit()** `auth(α) ∧ permitted(α,o,Σ) ∧ g_o(s) ∧ o(s)∈V ∧ D(s,o(s)) ∧ ⋀F(φ,s)` — **six independent refusals** (not known / not you / not now / not ever / not that way / not across this wall); auth ≠ permitted | I | 🔨 guard/result/privilege ✅; auth (login) app-layer ✅; **D ✅** (`transition.rs`); **F ✅ built 2026-08-17** (`flow.rs`, =CON-1); ★★ **the *not you* conjunct is now COMPLETE in both its forms — 2026-08-17**: ambient (`permitted`, 2026-08-12) and **capability-borne** (`capability.rs`, IMM-7), and the six refusals gained three more names rather than being collapsed — `skin_resolves`, `capability_designates`, `capability_carries`, because *not a member*, *too little authority*, *unreadable bundle*, *wrong object* and *right unheld* send whoever reads the log to five different repairs — *not across this wall* is now a real refusal: a crossing to a counterparty the firewall does not admit is refused at the gate with nothing committed |
| IMM-2 | **Defence in depth = ⊕** `admit* = ⋀_{H∈path} admit_H` (monotone, person-first) — no separate layering construct | I | ✅ **closed 2026-08-29 by CON-8** (`admissibility.rs`) — the row is the same claim from the Immune side, and the article is explicit that there is **no separate layering construct**, so building one here would have been the deviation. Monotone and order-independent are executed as tests; person-first survives as refusal-only-on-newly-caused |
| IMM-3 | **Complete mediation / reference monitor** — always invoked, tamper-proof, small; one unmediated path **deletes** the induction | II | ✅ **structural win** — Rust one write path (closes Python's opt-in gate + second write path) |
| IMM-4 | **Fail-closed, not fail-open** — an uncompilable invariant/post_constraint must **refuse or defer, never silently skip** (Python's fail-open, newly named in this article) | II | ✅ (R2 #8 — Rust refuses an unreadable rule) |
| IMM-5 | **Authentication** — PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 (260k iters, per-user salt, constant-time), JWT with `token_version` = **real revocation** | IV | ✅ app-layer (users.py) — solid, not core-engine |
| IMM-6 | **Authorization = per-membership-edge privileges** (authority on the relationship, not the person) + **`skins`** (named RBAC bundles); `min_privilege` = the per-action binding | IV | ✅ **complete 2026-08-17** — `min_privilege` enforced since 2026-08-12 (R2 #10, edge-based and monotone across the holon path; Python's inert model is REAL in Rust), and ★ **`skins` closed with IMM-7**: `Skin` had shipped as a struct `MembershipEdge` referenced and **nothing read** — *'a field, not a check'*, the exact defect this module's own header criticises `min_privilege` for in the reference. `SkinRegistry` now resolves it, and where an edge carries both a tier and a skin the **weaker wins** (`max`, the same weakest-link algebra as the path walk) so a bundle cannot be a promotion route. ★★ **An edge naming a skin nothing resolves is REFUSED** (`Denial::UnresolvedSkin`), never demoted to its raw tier — falling back is how a typo'd role (`gest` for `guest`) becomes owner-by-accident, and IMM-4's *an unreadable rule refuses, never silently skips* is the same rule one layer up. |
| IMM-7 | **Approval token** `guard ∧ (effect_class=sandbox ∨ valid_token(approve(o,principal)))`; today "human decides" holds by **incapacity, not mediation**; **confused-deputy risk** once Symbionts can write → pass authority *with* the request (capability) | IV | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`capability.rs`) — and ★★ **the row had THREE clauses, two of which were already built when this slice opened; the STATUS here was stale, not the code.** **(a) The approval token shipped 2026-08-12 as OPV-30/31** (`approval.rs`: `ApprovalToken` with no public constructor, `EffectClass::{Sandbox,Live,Unchecked}`, `NonceLedger`, wired into `execute_admitted` with binding/principal/expiry checked **before** the body and the nonce spent **at commit**, so a gate refusal never consumes an approval). This row read `⬜ not started` for five days after it landed. **(b) `permitted(α,o,Σ)` also shipped 2026-08-12** (`principal.rs`, tracked correctly under IMM-6) — so the *authorization half was not the gap either*. ★★ **(c) The genuinely unbuilt clause was the confused deputy**, and that is this slice. Hardy 1988: a deputy applies its own authority to a name someone else chose; a Symbiont acting on a parsed SMS or an Arena artefact is that shape by construction. ★★ **`Authorization::Capability` carries NO `&Memberships`** — Miller-Yee-Shapiro's *no ambient authority* made a property of the type, so a deputy holding a capability cannot fall back on the issuer's wider authority even by mistake. Proven as a pair at the gate: the same principal, the same untrusted input and the same operator **commit** under ambient authority and are **refused** (`capability_designates`, state byte-identical) under a capability — the vulnerability is asserted so the fix has something to be a fix of. ★★ **Redesignation is unrepresentable** (`Attenuation` carries no sustain, so the deputy's own move is absent from the vocabulary, not guarded against); amplification is **refused and named** rather than silently clamped; issuing is a **transfer not a mint** (it consults a real edge, so a stranger gets `NoEdge`) and reuses `effective_privilege_with`, so it cannot route around ⊕. **Unforgeability is structural** — no public fields, no constructor, no `Deserialize`, proven by a `compile_fail` doctest. **Residuals, named:** unforgeability is *in-process*, not cryptographic (a wire form needs a MAC/key → **host-layer slot** under ADR-0001, the CTL-6 relocation); **this is not revocation** (a capability is valid until dropped; revoking the underlying edge does not invalidate one already issued); and **nothing issues capabilities to Symbionts yet**. ★★★ **UPDATE 2026-08-18 — the fix is now IN FORCE on the reasoning path** (follow-on wiring 2): `Agent::act` runs every strategy node under `Authorization::Capability` from an attenuated warrant, and the warrant is a **required parameter**, so an agent cannot reason on ambient authority. What stays open is narrower than it was: **the core still issues nothing** — `Capability::issue` needs real `Memberships`, which are host state — so *who grants a household's capability* remains the host's, deliberately; and revocation is still unbuilt. |
| IMM-8 | **Verdict set {Admit, Refuse, Clamp, Defer}** — **Defer = the tolerance mechanism**; over-refusal → users build a bypass, so over-refusal is a *security* failure | VI | ✅ (R2 #11 refuse/clamp/defer) |
| IMM-9 | **Anti-over-refusal** — advisory-by-default, binding refuses only on **newly-caused** breach (stops a breached parent freezing all children) | VI | ✅ (R2 `_check_parent_binding_gate`) |
| IMM-10 | **Fixed vs learned rules** — `R_fixed` = non-authorable pre-gate ordered first (the OTP secret filter); `R_learned` = ParseRules with `trust`+`provenance`, gated adoption (self-match + regression) | V | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`learned.rs`) — ★★ **and STEP-0 settled the scope before anything was designed: the ParseRule system is PYTHON-ONLY.** `ParseRule`, `parse_rule`, `transducer`, `OTP` and `R_fixed` are all **grep-0** in this core, so the Rust work is *not* to port a subsystem across to have somewhere to hang a bound — it is the **generic mechanism §V is really about**, of which a ParseRule is one instance: the fixed/learned split, the ordering, and the least-privilege bound. The regex library, the seed set and the adoption verifier stay app-layer, said plainly rather than quietly imported. ★★ **`R_fixed` stays a fixed point, and it is structural rather than enforced:** `Edit::apply` is `(&Definition) -> Definition`, acting on `𝒮`/`Inv`/`T`, with **no parameter through which a rule set could enter and no return through which one could leave** — the same shape as EVT-15's finding about the patch fold. A `FixedRule` is not part of a `Definition`, so `∀e, ∀r ∈ R_fixed: e(r) = r`, exercised over all **eight** edit kinds and compared byte-for-byte rather than argued from the signature. ★ And a fixed rule carries **no operator and no capability** — a pre-gate refuses, it does not act, so asking for one is an `Err` rather than an empty capability that would read as *bounded* where the truth is *not applicable*. **Ordered-before is structural too**: `screen` is the only entry, the fixed tier runs first, and the order is not a parameter. ★★ **Gap 1 — trust is now a real decision input, and the finding was verified rather than quoted:** across **47** `trust` hits in the reference, **not one** is a comparison, branch, sort key or ordering (the only operator-shaped hit is the `<...>` in a docstring listing the tuple's fields). `TrustPolicy` is the evaluator, and it drives **two** decisions — **precedence** (a `Shipped` rule wins a match over a `ProposedConfirmed` one even when declared second) and **privilege** (a machine-synthesised, human-clicked-yes rule gets a strictly weaker capability than an authored one). ★ Both derive from **one** declared thing, so they cannot drift into two orderings that disagree, and `TrustPolicy` implements **no `Default`** — what a trust level ought to reach is a deployment's policy, and a library that invented one would decide it invisibly (the `Lateness` reason). ★★★ **Gap 2 — least privilege on the learned tier, the keystone, and it CONSUMES IMM-7:** `IngressBound` declares the operators reachable from ingest at all (small, declared, HRU), and a learned rule holds a capability **attenuated to that bound** rather than the ambient authority of whoever it acts for — the confused-deputy configuration IMM-7 built the capability for, with the learned tier as the deputy. Proven **end to end at the real gate**: an ingest-tier rule invoking an out-of-bound operator reaches `execute_as` and is refused with `capability_carries`, no mutations, no events, state byte-identical — *and the in-bound call still commits*, asserted in the same test so the bound cannot quietly become an outage. ★★★ **No trust level lifts the bound** — asserted across all three levels, because a ceiling with an exception is not a ceiling — and ★ the bound **never exceeds the principal it acts for**, since a capability is a transfer: the policy sets a ceiling, never a floor. **Residuals:** no matcher library (substring only, deliberately — a richer one would invite porting the reference's regexes); **adoption gating is not built here** (verify + regression need the matcher and the stored corpus, both app-layer, and both already exist there); adoption and authority are kept **separate** on purpose, so a rule outside the bound is adopted-and-visible rather than silently dropped; and **nothing hands the capability to a running rule engine**, because this core has no ingest loop — the same threading residual IMM-7 already carries. |
| IMM-11 | **Layered controls = independence of failure** (not count) — the 3 OTP layers share **one** detector (10 hand-synced regexes) → add a **different-kind** structural detector | III | ✅ **DONE (core)** — `core/secret_shape.rs`, 13 tests, wired into `parse_message` ahead of every rule. ★★★ **Reads no words at all**: a bare 4–8 digit code (necessary, not sufficient — a real paybill confirmation genuinely contains a bare account number) plus 3 of 4 corroborating shape features (no transaction reference, brief, a shouted 3-word run, no figure quoted in cents). Combined with the vocabulary by **OR**, because the costs are asymmetric — a false positive is one capture somebody re-enters, a false negative is a live credential in a database. **Proven independent, not asserted**: a credential message in Swahili passes the ten regexes untouched and is refused on shape; a wordy warning with no code at all is missed by shape and caught by vocabulary. ★★ **Calibrated against the real corpus** — the real OTP sample *names a USD amount*, so the obvious "no money mentioned" rule would have missed it outright; it is caught on `no_cents` instead. All 8 real transaction shapes are a standing negative control. ★★ **A real bug the corpus found**: reading the full stop in `000000.` as a decimal point made the detector miss a real TAN code. ⚠ **The Android copy is NOT updated** — see the branch note; until it is, this layer protects the store, not the wire. |
| IMM-12 | **Detection over the log** — anomaly/negative-selection; **base-rate limit** (output must be *attention* not *denial* → route to Defer); **heartbeats** = declared expectation checked deterministically (no base-rate problem) | VII | ⬜ **NOT started, correctly LAST** (a detector over an unmediated system reports a hole it can't close) |
| IMM-13 | **Forged credentials** — Sybil (grant must be a **transfer, not a mint**), unsigned Arena artefacts (**content hash + signature**; integrity ≠ authenticity ≠ quality) | VIII | ⏸️ economy-deferred |

**Summary:** the security *shape* is right, and Rust closed most *evaluation* gaps structurally — complete mediation (one path), fail-closed, refuse/clamp/defer, enforced privilege all ✅. **Pending:** transition/flow constraints (=M-CON), a second-kind OTP detector, and detection/heartbeats (built **last**) — **both app-layer**. Sybil-grant + Arena-signing are economy-deferred.

**Update 2026-08-17 — IMM-7 closed, and the reconcile that opened it is worth keeping.** ★★ The row named three clauses and **two were already built**: the approval token (OPV-30/31, 2026-08-12) and `permitted(α,o,Σ)` with per-edge privileges (`principal.rs`, the same day, tracked under IMM-6). The status line said `⬜ not started` for five days after the first of those landed, so STEP-0 corrected **the tracker, not the code** — and corrected the slice brief too, which expected authorization to be the gap. ★★ The genuinely unbuilt clause was **the confused deputy** (Hardy 1988), and it is the one that matters once Symbionts can write: an operative acts on a person's authority over inputs *it did not choose*, so ambient authority applies everything that person holds to a name from untrusted text. The fix is Dennis & Van Horn's — designation and rights in one unforgeable token — with Miller-Yee-Shapiro's sharpening made **structural**: `Authorization::Capability` carries **no `&Memberships`**, so the ambient fallback is *absent* rather than merely unused. ★ Three properties are unrepresentable rather than checked: **redesignation** (no sustain on `Attenuation`), **widening rights** (no such method), and **forging one** (no `Deserialize`, proven by a `compile_fail` doctest). Asking for a stronger *tier* is representable because a caller may sincerely ask, and is **refused and named** — silently clamping would hide that someone asked for more than they had, the trap `admission.rs` warns about a layer down. ★ The same slice closed **IMM-6's `skins`** — declared 2026-08-12 and read by nothing, which is *'a field, not a check'* turned back on this codebase — with the unresolvable case **failing closed**. ★★ **The divergence's counterweight is two things and the first is genuine parity**: authentication is the reference's (PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, 260k iterations, per-user salt, constant-time, JWT with `token_version` = real revocation) and nothing here improves on it — which is exactly §IV's point that `auth` and `permitted` are separate conjuncts; and SUS-7's boundary `μ` is at parity on both sides, which is what gives a membership edge something to be an edge *of*. ★ **The reference's one real ownership check is credited precisely**: `_assert_owns_sustain()` 404s for both not-found and not-owned so ids cannot be enumerated — careful work, and **single-owner, binary, and at the route layer** rather than a conjunct of `admit()`. ★★ **The grep that would most mislead a re-reader is named**: `skin` returns **16 hits and zero real** — every one is the substring in *asking*, the sharpest false positive since CTL-11's `gain`/*again*; and `permitted`'s 11 hits are the **allow-list**, which is §IV's conflation stated out loud (`sustain_engine.py:1618`, *'operator is permitted by this sustain's spec'*). **Residuals:** in-process unforgeability only (a wire form needs a key → host-layer slot); **no revocation**; and **nothing issues capabilities to Symbionts yet**. ★★★ **The threading this named as *the sequenced next step* SHIPPED 2026-08-18** (follow-on wiring 2): a strategy is now a real holder of an attenuated capability, refused at the gate when it reaches outside it, and refused at attenuation when it asks for more than the household holds. The confused-deputy fix is **in force where it mattered most** — the deputy the article names. Still open: no wire form, no revocation, and **the core issues no capability itself**, since `Capability::issue` needs host-held `Memberships`.

**Update 2026-08-17 — IMM-10 closed, and with it M-IMM's ENGINE HALF.** ★★ STEP-0 settled the scope first: **the ParseRule system is Python-only** (`ParseRule`/`parse_rule`/`transducer`/`OTP`/`R_fixed` all grep-0 here), so the Rust work is the **generic mechanism** §V is about rather than a port — the split, the ordering, and the bound; the regex library, the seed set and the adoption verifier stay app-layer. ★★ **The fixed tier stays a fixed point structurally**: `Edit::apply` is `(&Definition) -> Definition` and a `FixedRule` is not part of a `Definition`, so no edit's range includes it — exercised over all eight edit kinds. Ordered-before is structural too (one entry point, order not a parameter), and a fixed rule holds **no capability at all**, because a pre-gate refuses rather than acts. ★★ **Trust became a real input, and §V's finding was verified rather than trusted**: across **47** `trust` mentions in the reference, **zero** are a comparison, branch, sort key or ordering. `TrustPolicy` now drives **precedence** and **privilege** from one declared mapping, with no `Default` — the meaning of a trust level is a deployment's policy, not a library's. ★★★ **And the keystone consumed IMM-7 exactly as intended**: the learned tier is a **deputy**, so it holds a capability attenuated to a declared `IngressBound` instead of ambient authority. An ingest-tier rule invoking an out-of-bound operator is refused **at the real gate** (`capability_carries`, state byte-identical) while the in-bound call still commits. **No trust level lifts the bound**, and the bound never exceeds the principal it acts for — a ceiling, never a floor. ★★ **The counterweight is the strongest in the module and it belongs to the reference**: the fixed/learned *split* is at parity, and the fixed tier there is its best security work — `contains_sensitive_secret()` runs **first in `parse_message()`** (before any parser, so no learned rule can see an OTP first) **and again in `capture()`** (before the raw text is ever written, so a rejected message leaves no row and no `raw_payload`), non-authorable, with a mirrored Java copy on the device so the secret never leaves the phone. Nothing here improves on any of it. ★ Adoption gating (verify + regression) is real there too. ★ **Two greps worth carrying**: `trust`'s **47 hits with zero decisions** (the count reads like a live subsystem and is entirely storage and display), and `R_fixed`'s **grep-0 that is not an absence** — the name appears nowhere while the thing is excellent, so a reviewer grepping the name would conclude the opposite of the truth. **What remains in M-IMM is app-layer only:** IMM-11 (a different-*kind* OTP detector — layering is independence of failure, not count) and IMM-12 (detection over the log, correctly last).

### Layer 3 — BOUNDARY

#### M-ING · Ingest & external integrations (RECEPTOR)  ✅ read
*Article: `3B1B — Ingest - Formal Execution.md` (RECEPTOR). B seen from outside: a total transducer with the gate behind it. Once past it, a sensor reading and a hand-typed spend are the same kind of thing.*

**Status: heavily built (Slices 5/11/13/14 — transducer, parse rules, one door, egress). Key gaps: heartbeats, device-as-Sustain, intrinsic-id key.**

| # | Feature | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| ING-1 | **Boundary as sensor** `τ:R→C` / `σ:E→R`; a transducer produces a **candidate `C≠S`**, not state; **no ingest write path** (same gate + fold as an internal write) | I·VI | ✅ structural (capture→execute_operator) |
| ING-2 | **Total τ, five-way result** `Mapped | ParsedUnmapped | Informational | Unparsed | Rejected`; **"I don't know" is a value, not a drop**; fail-safe default = **surface, not drop** | II | ✅ app-layer (transducer.py, deterministic) |
| ING-3 | **Secret pre-gate** — `secret(r)` **first, before persistence**; content-based; **fixed, non-authorable**; two independent impls (device + server) | III | ✅ app-layer (before INSERT + device twin) — the IMM-10 R_fixed |
| ING-4 | **Sender-strict dispatch** — source decided by the **sender**, never the wording (`argmax textual-fit` forbidden) | IV | ✅ app-layer (`_PARSERS_BY_SOURCE`) |
| ING-5 | **Idempotent intake** — at-least-once ∘ idempotent = exactly-once **effect**; key `⟺ same fact`; **intrinsic id (txn code) first**, text-hash fallback (a UUID partitions by *arrivals* — always wrong); duplicate = no-op | V | 🔨 substrate dedupe ✅ (R2 #18); **intrinsic-id-as-key ⬜** |
| ING-6 | **One write path** `commit(Mapped)=execute_operator`; mapped-but-invalid is **quarantined (named rule), never forced**; message id stamped on the event | VI | ✅ (Rust one path; refusal quarantines) |
| ING-7 | **Parse rules as declared primitives** — `Γ⊢r` (pattern compiles ∧ status=mapped ⟹ operator∈Registry ∧ params⊆declared); ordered first-match; examples = regression corpus | VII | ✅ app-layer (parse_rule.py, 22 shapes as data) |
| ING-8 | **★ Liveness / staleness / heartbeats** — `staleness>θ(c)`, `θ=Percentile_p(gaps)`; **heartbeats convert a statistical detector into a deterministic one** (`≤h+δ`); never guess a cadence; seen when it *speaks*, not when it parses | VIII | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`liveness.rs` + the host) — ★★★ **A capture path that dies is silent, and silence is also what a quiet Tuesday looks like.** A revoked SMS permission, a rule that stopped matching, a bank that changed its sender id — all three present as *no new messages*, and nothing else in the system can tell, because the failure is an absence. ★★★ *Never guess a cadence* is **structural**: `learned_theta` returns `None` below five gaps, so a source nobody has watched long enough is **unknown** — not healthy, which would hide a dead path, and not stale, which would cry wolf about a source that is simply new. A declared heartbeat wins over a learned θ (a promise beats an estimate) and needs no warm-up. ★★ The threshold is **nearest-rank**, so the number shown is a gap the source really had rather than an average of two it never did. ★★ *Seen when it speaks* was already right in the host and is now asserted: a message nobody could parse still proves the phone is on, and counting only successful parses would send somebody to check the phone when the answer is *the format changed* |
| ING-9 | **Device-as-Sustain** `Σ_dev` (connectivity/battery/**queue_depth**/t_last_ack); staleness = ordinary invariant; **queue_depth = the leading health metric**; a liveness invariant is evaluated by the **receiving** Sustain (a dead device can't self-report) | IX | ✅ **DONE (core)** — `core/device.rs`, 18 tests, composing ING-8 (`liveness`) and ING-10 (`outbox::queue_depth`). ★★★ **`Reading::FromTheDevice` has NO liveness field to read** — a type-level statement that a self-report cannot establish its own aliveness, and `is_stale()` returns `Option` so the device's own reading answers `None`. `t_last_ack` is written by the receiver from its own clock. ★★★ **Staleness really is an ordinary invariant**: the device's state is a plain `Value` and its region is plain `Interval`s, so `Region::distance` measures a phone with no line written for phones — `queue_depth ≤ max` is a bound like any other rather than alarm code beside the ingest path. ★★★ **A test found a real design error**: a first draft claimed a missing dimension "simply is not bounded"; `Region` correctly refuses to measure a distance it has no number for. So a device that stopped reporting its battery is `Health::Unmeasurable`, **neither fine nor drifting** — calling it healthy is how a silent failure reads as a quiet week. Only a *missing reading* becomes `Unmeasurable`; a malformed region stays an error, or a declaration bug would hide behind a phone. ★★ Never having spoken is `Unknown`, not stale. Battery is bounded **below only** — there is no such thing as too much charge. ⬜ Host wiring (`ingest_sources` remains a plain table) is a separate integration pass. |
| ING-10 | **Ack + transactional outbox** — ack = **durably committed**; retry backoff+jitter; queue-depth reporting | V·IX | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`outbox.rs`) — ★★★ **`Unknown` is the whole reason the module exists.** A request that times out has told you nothing: treating it as failure drops real transactions, treating it as success loses them more quietly. Keeping it is safe **only** because capture is idempotent — which is what idempotency was for. ★★★ **An ack means durably committed, not received**: a server that acks on receipt and crashes has told a phone to forget something that exists nowhere, and the phone is the only other copy. ★★★ **Jitter without randomness** — the core has no random source, and deriving it from the message id turns out better than a random one: two phones do not thunder together *and* the schedule is reproducible, so "why did it retry then?" has an answer. Full jitter (`[0, exponential]`), capped. **Queue depth leads** because it moves on the first failed send, where staleness needs a whole interval to pass and a missing transaction needs somebody to go looking |
| ING-11 | **Egress asymmetry** — can't make someone else's receiver idempotent → **irreversibility declared**, no blind auto-retry; **human gate** (approval token = a term inside `admit()`) | X | ✅ **DONE** — `core/egress.rs`, 14 tests. ★★★ **The same `Unknown` that `outbox.rs` retries on is the one this stops on, and both are right** — the difference is entirely who owns the receiver; asserted as a pair in one test so the asymmetry is proven rather than described. **No blind auto-retry on anything not declared undoable**, and `Reversibility::Unknown` counts as not-undoable: the two mistakes are not symmetric (an interruption vs somebody's money), so an unclassified egress gets the cautious answer. `Reversible` **carries the compensating act**, because "undoable" with no undo named is the claim a retry would be relying on. Even a safe retry is bounded. ★★ **There is no `NeedsApproval` refusal and the absence is the finding**: `EffectClass` has no *live and unapproved* variant, so a caller cannot reach the gate in that state — writing the arm would put a refusal in the vocabulary that no input can produce. (approval-token-in-admit half = IMM-7, done 2026-08-17) |
| ING-12 | **Cross-source correlation** — one transfer → two senders' SMS → two candidates; needs a fact-based key (amount + shared M-PESA ref) | IV | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`correlation.rs` + the host join) — ★★★ **the host was already joining, on the REFERENCE ALONE**, which is not a fact: reference codes are allocated by different systems and collide, and when they did, a real separate transaction was silently treated as already-applied and **vanished without trace** — nobody finds out, because the whole effect of the join is that nothing is shown. The key is now reference **and** amount; an unknown amount falls back rather than refusing, because a missed join shows a duplicate a person can undo while a wrong join hides a transaction nobody knows to look for. ★★ The core module **surfaces** rather than suppresses, naming both messages: no text-hash fallback across sources (two descriptions of one event differ by definition), and opposite directions are evidence FOR rather than against |
| ING-13 | **`t_event` at the boundary** — lift the SMS's own date/time into event time (today only `received_at`, so skew isn't computable) | VIII | ✅ **done 2026-08-29** (`event_time.rs` + the ingest door) — ★★★ **the patterns had been capturing `date` and `time` all along and throwing them away.** 32 fields across 20 rules are now kept; nothing about what a rule MATCHES changed. Without `t_event` skew was not merely unknown but **unmeasurable**: a backfill of two thousand texts stamped a month of spending with one afternoon, and nothing could tell because there was no second reading to disagree with the first. ★★★ Three ways to be wrong by a lot, each pinned: **`12 AM` is midnight and `12 PM` is noon** (a twelve-hour error puts an evening payment on the wrong day); **`20/7/26` is day-first**, settled by real samples where 20 cannot be a month (the other reading fails invisibly for two thirds of every month); and **an unreadable date yields `None`, never *now*** — *now* makes skew exactly zero, the one value that looks healthy, for precisely the messages nobody could read. ★★ The UTC offset is the host's declaration about its own senders, not a locale the core assumes |

**Summary:** Ingest is among the most-built modules — transducer, five-way result, secret pre-gate, sender-strict dispatch, single door, parse-rules-as-data, human-gated egress all ✅. **Residue: heartbeats** (would have caught the outage), **device-as-Sustain** (queue-depth), the **intrinsic-id dedup key**, cross-source correlation, and the approval-token term.

### Layer 4 — COGNITION

#### M-MON · Monitor  ✅ read  ★ SPINE (observe)
*Article: `3B1B — Monitor - Formal Execution.md` (PERCEPT). Observe true state under noise; escalate only when the math demands. CUSUM is the formal Monitor→Controller boundary.*

**Status: the detection chain, the per-sustain ENGINE that owns it, and the escalation into the OODA loop are all built (2026-08-12, 2026-08-16) — OBSERVE is self-driving. Still ⬜: the belief tracker (MON-8), the preattentive encoder (MON-7), observability/traces/stream windows, and harmonics (MON-13, waiting on nothing but its own build).**

| # | Feature | § | Rust status |
|---|---|---|---|
| MON-1 | **Observability** — `is_observable()` (observability matrix rank = n); partial when rank<n → distribution not point; **"unmeasured state is ungoverned state"** | I | ✅ **done 2026-08-17 via the Sustena-native form, with the MATRIX DECLINED** (`observability.rs`). ★★ **§I's `rank([C; CA; …; CAⁿ⁻¹]) = n` is a DECLINED IMPORT, not a gap** — the same position MON-2 takes on the Kalman filter, resting on MON-11's ratified caveat that *the observability and Kalman apparatus of §I–II assumes a continuous linear system*. ★ And the concrete reason beyond the categorical one: **`CAᵏ` needs a single `A` to take powers of, and there is not one** — the dynamics here is a *choice* (which operator someone runs), not a fixed linear map, so any `A` would have to be picked and picking one would be a fabrication. This row's own warning settles it: *a rough answer to a rank question is worse than none.* ★★ **What ships is the discrete analogue, and it is a translation rather than an approximation:** `C` → dimensions a declared source measures; `A` → one operator (reads `X`, writes `Y` ⇒ `X → Y`); `CAᵏ` → a measurement reachable in `k` operator hops; `rank(O)=n` → every governed dimension reaches a measurement. The verdict **names the operator chain** that carries a value, so the claim is checkable rather than taken on trust, and the search is breadth-first because *which block row* is a question about the smallest `k`. ★★ **Conservative where the effects are unsummarised, and it says so:** writes come from OP-3's optional `EffectSummary`, so an operator without one contributes **no edges** — a dimension can read unobservable that a summary would have shown observable. That is the safe direction (the Goodhart support-matching discipline), and `Observability::Unobservable` **carries the operators whose summaries were missing** with `is_conservative()` to say whether the verdict rests on them, so *unobservable* and *we could not see* stay distinguishable. ★ **Over today's registry that is the whole picture** — no shipped operator declares a summary, so every non-measured dimension reads conservative, asserted so a future summarised operator breaks the test and forces a look. ★ **Where it meets MON-8:** §I's *partial observability → a distribution, not a point* is the belief, and this row says **which** dimensions need one (`requiring_belief`). And the distinction from `ungoverned()` is kept sharp rather than called an extension: that is a **runtime** question (*has anything spoken yet* — answerable by waiting), this is a **structural** one (*could anything ever* — waiting will not help). **Residuals:** root-name granularity (coarse in the *observable* direction, the less safe one, and stated); a path is not an estimator (reachability does not say how to invert the chain); and a path is not an estimator.  ★★ **WIRED into `MonitorEngine` 2026-08-17** (the Phase-2 consolidation) — declared per sustain on `SustainWatch`, `Option` and absent by default so a sustain declaring none behaves exactly as before. ★★ **Runs on demand, and is structural** — `MonitorEngine::observability(sustain, registry, sources)` reads the **operator graph**, which this sustain's state cannot change, so running it per tick would burn work on an answer that cannot have moved. The governed set is the region's own declared intervals, and over today's registry the report says **conservative** per finding rather than in a footnote. |
| MON-2 | **Kalman filter** — optimal estimate under noise (predict/update, gain K); show `x_hat` not raw | II | ⬜ **deliberately not built** — MON-11's fit caveat is now discharged *without* it: the Sustena-native form (distance-to-V on the fold + CUSUM/EWMA) ships as of 2026-08-12. Kalman assumes a continuous linear ODE, which discrete event-sourced state is not, so this is a **declined import**, not a gap. ★ **Re-confirmed 2026-08-17 at the one place it would have been easiest to slip in:** §VIII's belief-state pseudocode calls `kalman_filter.update(observation)` by name, and MON-8 shipped **without it** — the `point_mass` half is served by EVT-10's snapshot collapse instead, which is the Sustena-native rule for what an observation of an external authority means. ★★ **And re-confirmed a second time the same day by MON-1**, which declined §I's *observability matrix* on the identical grounds — MON-11's caveat names the two together (*'the observability matrix and the Kalman filter are a loose import'*), so declining one and importing the other would have been incoherent. The declination now has **three** concrete sites: the detection pipeline, the belief state, and the observability analysis. |
| MON-3 | **Three pillars** — Metrics `(name,value,t,labels)`; Logs (ordered append-only); Traces (span DAG; `Span`, `TraceCollector`) | III | ✅ **DONE** — `core/telemetry.rs`, 15 tests. ★★★ **Metrics and traces are FOLDS over the log, never stores beside it.** Three separate pipelines is the industry shape and here it would be the state-cache mistake a second time: a counter written beside the log by the same code can disagree with it, and nothing can say which is wrong. Nothing here is stored, so there is nothing to drift *from*. The cost is named rather than hidden — every read is a scan; when that stops being right the answer is a cache that is provably a fold, not a second writer. ★★★ **A span has no duration, because the log records instants and inventing one would be a fabricated measurement that looks like evidence.** The available honest reading is `elapsed_ms` — how long the world took to finish reacting to a cause — which is explicitly not CPU time. ★★★ **`TraceCollector` was declined**: `causes` is recorded when events are written, so a collector would be a second recording of a fact the log already holds. ★★ Multiple `causes` are kept (forcing a tree invents a causality nobody recorded); a cause outside the window is reported as an **orphan**, never reparented to root (a window boundary is not an origin); `LabelOf` is an enum so a free-text label cannot be declared by accident, and `cardinality()` is askable because cardinality failures are not caught by any series being wrong. |
| MON-4 | **Stream processing** — tumbling/sliding/session windows; `SlidingWindowAggregator` (count/rate/by_type/error_rate); watermarks + late-event policy | IV | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`windowing.rs`) — the typology, on top of EVT-6's watermarks + lateness (same day). ★★ **Tumbling is not a third mechanism and is not rebuilt.** `[kT, (k+1)T)` **is** a half-open period partition, so calendar-aligned tumbling is `tumbling_from_recurrence`, a **one-line delegate** to EVT-11's `Recurrence::expand` — which matters concretely rather than aesthetically, since a month is not a fixed number of milliseconds and a duplicate would have had to get calendar length right a second time or quietly get it wrong. And **fixed-duration tumbling is the degenerate sliding case where `step == size`**: a conformance case checks `Sliding{DAY, DAY}` and an EVT-11 daily expansion produce **byte-identical** windows, so the claim is measured rather than argued. `Sliding::is_tumbling()` merely *reports* which case a spec is. ★★ **Sliding overlaps honestly:** size 60m stepping 15m gives an `overlap_factor` of 4, and `windows_containing` returns a `Vec` — an event is counted in **each** window containing it, because that is what makes a rolling rate roll. A **`step > size` is refused** (`StepExceedsSize`), since gaps between windows would silently drop every event that fell in one. ★★ **Session is data-driven, and its end is not known until the gap is.** A session ends **τ after its own last event**, not at the event that revealed the gap; the last session in a batch is `SessionState::Open` — *not* an empty session and *not* a closed one, the same *we cannot answer yet* EVT-6's `Closing::NotYet` reports. ★★ **All three close on a watermark, never a clock.** Sliding and tumbling windows **are** EVT-6 `Window`s, so `is_closed_by` is inherited rather than re-implemented. A session is the case that could have gone wrong — its end is unknown in advance — so `close_on` takes a **`&Watermark` and nothing else**: a processing clock two hours past the gap carrying an honest 90-minute bound does **not** close it. **A clock is not evidence**, and there is no *expire after a while* path, which would be the wall clock wearing a different name. ★ **This row's own wording corrected, second time:** it names `SlidingWindowAggregator (count/rate/by_type/error_rate)` as if shipped, and it is **grep-0 on both sides**. Aggregation is a **consumer** of the typology, not part of it — building one in would tie a window shape to a particular thing being summed — so it stays deliberately unbuilt and the wording is corrected rather than satisfied. **Residuals:** sub-daily fixed-`T` tumbling routes through `Sliding` because EVT-11's grammar has no `HOURLY` (that row's named residual, not closed here); `sessionise` **reads the order it is handed** (as `fold` does — `event::order` exists for the caller); no session merging across batches.  ★★ **WIRED into `MonitorEngine` 2026-08-17** (the Phase-2 consolidation) — declared per sustain on `SustainWatch`, `Option` and absent by default so a sustain declaring none behaves exactly as before. ★ **Runs on demand**, over the timed readings the engine records — `windows()` and `readings_in()`. The engine holds readings rather than an event log, and **only a timed reading can be placed in a window**: a pass through `ingest` carries no event time, and putting it in one would be inventing it. |
| MON-5 | **EWMA smoothing** `α·y+(1−α)·EWMA`, half-life ≈0.693/α; per-widget α | V | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (`detect.rs`, 2026-08-12) — `EWMA[k] = α·y[k] + (1−α)·EWMA[k−1]`, seeded on the first observation (starting from zero would fabricate a ramp-up transient in the one signal everything trusts). **`α` is a declared per-detector field**, not a constant — *a budget ring can be cosmetically smooth; a constraint-health indicator must respond within seconds* — and `half_life()` states what the chosen value actually buys, since *"α = 0.1"* says little and *"t½ ≈ 7 updates"* says what it costs |
| MON-6 | **CUSUM detector** — `S⁺/S⁻` sums; fires on **persistent** shift; `classify()`→CRITICAL/WARNING (→Controller) vs INFO (stay); **the formal Monitor→Controller boundary** | VI | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (`detect.rs`, 2026-08-12) — **the formal Monitor→Controller boundary now exists.** `S⁺`/`S⁻` per §VI, with **μ₀, δ and h declared** (same discipline as the region weights: `δ` is the smallest shift worth detecting, `h` is how much evidence before interrupting someone). `Severity::escalates()` IS the seam — CRITICAL/WARNING cross to the Controller, INFO stays in the Monitor — and it is what **CTL-7** will consume. **The proof of value is pinned by a vector, not prose:** a sustained small drift alerts, while a spike **4.5× larger** that corrects never does. ⚠ **The article's pseudocode zeroes `S_pos` BEFORE `classify()` reads it**, so every upward shift would report INFO and never escalate; implemented per the prose (excess captured at the alert, then reset) and recorded rather than silently fixed |
| MON-7 | **Preattentive visual encoding** — map data dims → hue/size/motion/orientation/brightness; `WidgetVisualEncoder`; Tufte data-ink | VII | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`preattentive.rs`) — **the door into the surface block**, and it establishes that block's conventions. ★★ **The *subject to* clause is a TYPE:** §VII's model maximises discriminability **subject to** the attributes coming only from the preattentive set, and `Channel` has exactly seven variants with **no `Other(String)`** — so an encoding that reached for a non-preattentive channel (a number to read, a label to parse) **cannot be written down**. That closure is load-bearing rather than decorative: leaking onto a non-preattentive channel does not degrade the glance, it ends it. ★★★ **Urgency is the household's, not the widget's, and three things make it structural:** `VisualSpec` has **no public constructor and no setters** (a widget cannot build itself a bright spec because it cannot build a spec at all); **the map is FIXED, not a parameter** (a configurable map is a gaming surface — whoever chose it could route urgency onto a channel nobody looks at); and ★★★ **brightness is field-relative**, with `encode_field` the only entry point. The consequence is the property worth having: **a widget can only get brighter by being WORSE** — asserted in both directions, since a peer moving further from `V` makes it **dimmer** while it is unchanged. Attention is zero-sum across the field and cannot be manufactured. That is UI-13's *salience is never rendered as salience* enforced at the encoder, and the encoding-side twin of Flight 401: where the Curated UI stops a loud-but-safe widget **winning a slot**, this stops it **looking urgent** once it has one. ★★ **Discriminability is RUN, not claimed** — the necessary condition (distinct state-classes encode distinctly) is asserted over every class this encoder can see, with an identical peer held constant so the peer cannot be what makes them differ; `Hue` has three variants so *two near-identical hues* is unrepresentable. The psychophysical half is **named as out of reach** rather than smuggled in. ★ **Encoder, not renderer:** `Hue::Amber` never `#E8A020`, a `Fraction` never a pixel radius — asserted by checking no `#`/`px`/`rgb`/`hsl` appears in a spec. And Tufte is honoured by **leaving saturation and enclosure unassigned**: there is no sixth or seventh data dimension, and giving them a value would be decoration wearing an encoding's clothes. ★ **It consumes the real engine**: a conformance case declares a real `Region`, runs a real `MonitorEngine` pass, and encodes its `Ingested` — so *this encodes `d(s,V)`* is checked against the thing that computes it. **Residuals:** the psychophysics is not checkable here; the map is deliberately non-configurable; a one-item field carries **no brightness and no size** (nothing to be brighter than, and `1.0` would be a fabricated maximum); nothing calls this from the engine yet, and should not — encoding has a surface's cadence, and a per-ingest encode would be a field of one; and trend is a **one-step** reading (`w` against its own EWMA level), since fitting a slope is MON-3's territory and would be the fabricated-model trap CTL-11 declined. |
| MON-8 | **Belief state (POMDP)** — `BeliefStateTracker`: Kalman point-mass on observation, `predict_forward`+`widen` on silence; `SENSOR_SILENT` past threshold | VIII | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`belief.rs`) — **Phase 2 opens here.** ★★ **The observation update is EVT-10's snapshot collapse, NOT a Kalman filter.** §VIII's pseudocode reads `point_mass(kalman_filter.update(observation))`; the Kalman half is **MON-2, the declined import** (see that row), and what survives is the `point_mass` — for which Sustena already has a rule: §VII's *a newer reading of an external authority supersedes*. `Belief` **holds an EVT-10 `SnapshotDim`** and collapses through it, so the coherence is structural rather than parallel and *'an observation arrived'* has one meaning in this codebase. The visible consequence is a real behaviour: **a stale reading arriving late does not collapse the belief** (`Collapse::Stale`), losing on `(τ,id)` exactly as it would on the state dimension. ★★ **Silence widens without inventing dynamics.** `σ = elapsed × DRIFT_RATE`, `variance = σ²` — §VIII taken literally, asserted **monotonic across 24 consecutive hours** rather than at one convenient point. `predict_forward` is only honest where a transition model exists, so `Dynamics` is **declared with no `Default`**: `Unknown` **holds the mean** and grows only the band (*we know less*, not *we know something else*), `LinearDrift` moves it only where someone said how. Fabricating a model to fill the pseudocode's shape would put a made-up trajectory behind a real-looking mean. ★ **The escalation is an admission, not an alarm.** A `SilenceAlert` carries the variance as its *reason* and rides the **existing** boundary — `detect::Severity::escalates()` — but is deliberately **not** a `detect::Alert`, because that type carries a `Shift` and **nothing shifted**: the boundary is reused, the shape is not. And it **can never be `Info`** — the one severity that would contradict §VIII is refused at declaration. ★ **§I is served, not claimed:** `ungoverned()` answers the concrete half that needs no matrix — a declared-governed dimension no sensor has ever spoken about. **Residuals:** not a joint POMDP belief (the tractable per-dimension mean+variance §VIII's own render asks for); the point-mass collapse **assumes the observation is authoritative**, which for a noisy sensor is a modelling assumption made and not checked — and modelling measurement noise is precisely where Kalman would come in, so the trap and the limit are the same decision seen twice; the drift rate is declared, not learned; and the point-mass collapse assumes the observation is authoritative.  ★★ **WIRED into `MonitorEngine` 2026-08-17** (the Phase-2 consolidation) — declared per sustain on `SustainWatch`, `Option` and absent by default so a sustain declaring none behaves exactly as before. ★★ **Runs per tick — but only through `ingest_at`**: a belief collapses on an *observation*, which needs an event time, and `ingest` has none to give. Rather than invent one (no clock in core), `ingest_at` is the door that carries a time and `ingest` **documents that it updates no belief** — a caller that never uses it has beliefs that were never observed, which is exactly what `ungoverned()` says about them. The engine offers every dimension the region declares, through `BeliefTracker::observe`, so **a stale reading does not collapse a belief inside the engine** for the same reason it does not on the state dimension. `silent(now)`/`silence_observations(now)` are **queries at a time**, not per-tick work. |
| MON-9 | **MonitorEngine** — per-sustain filters/detectors/trackers; `ingest()` pipeline **Kalman→EWMA→CUSUM + belief + Controller.escalate on WARNING+**; `render_widget()`, `tick()` | IX | ✅ **the engine, `ingest()` and the escalation are built 2026-08-16** (`monitor.rs` · `MonitorEngine`, `SustainWatch`, `flatten_holarchy`, `ingest`, `drive`) — **and OBSERVE is now self-driving**: an escalating ingest hands an `OodaObservation` straight to the OODA loop (CTL-3), which is what the chain lacked. The pipeline is the **native** one, `W = d(s,V) → EWMA → CUSUM` — **no Kalman stage**, per the Additions' fit caveat (= MON-2, a declined import, not a gap this leaves open). Per-sustain `α`/`μ₀`/`δ`/`h`/`θ_fire`, so one household's drift cannot move another's accumulator. §III's *"the Monitor is always running"* is honoured structurally: watching continues while the loop is parked mid-cycle and `held_back` names the phase that declined the hand-off. **Deliberately NOT built and NOT stubbed** — the belief tracker (= MON-8), `render_widget()`/the visual encoder (= MON-7), and `tick()` (the host's driver, same boundary CTL-9 draws). Disclosed: history is retained un-truncated, because dropping the oldest would silently change what a bounded `Never` concludes |
| MON-10 | **★ Urgency = distance-to-V** — `urgency(s)=d(s,V)`; **Monitor computes it**; consumed by Curated UI (salience) + Controller (escalation); ungameable (no second notion of "important") | Add. | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (`region.rs`, 2026-08-12) — `urgency(s) = d(s,V)` is real, and it is **the** salience quantity: a fact about the household's own declared region, so it cannot be gamed by a widget or an operative competing for attention. Replaces the `pct = spent/allocated` proxy **and its concrete defect** — the reference returns `0.0` whenever `allocated <= 0`, reporting an overspent unfunded pocket as maximally calm; as a region it is simply outside V by exactly the overspend. Consumed by M-UI (salience, UI-7) and M-CTL (escalation, CTL-7) |
| MON-11 | **Sustena-native form (fit caveat)** — Kalman/observability assume a continuous linear ODE; Sustena state is discrete/event-sourced → native form is **distance-to-V on the fold, with CUSUM/EWMA over that series** (same boundary, no ODE) | Add. | ✅ **DONE in Rust** (2026-08-12) — **the native form is complete**: distance-to-V computed on the fold, with **CUSUM/EWMA over that series**, and **no ODE assumed anywhere**. That is the article's fit caveat discharged end to end — the observability/Kalman apparatus stays a loose import, deliberately not built (see MON-2) |
| MON-12 | **Watching metered, looking spends a budget** — surfacing spends a working-memory slot (~4) + pawa; the CUSUM crossing is economic, not only statistical | Add. | ⏸️ economy-adjacent |
| MON-13 | **Frequency-domain change (harmonics)** — read the fold in the frequency domain (DFT over a windowed series) to separate a genuine shift from an expected cycle | Add. | ✅ **done 2026-08-17** (`harmonics.rs`). ★★ **A correction to this row's own prior text, found by reading before building:** it said this *'consumes `signal.rs` rather than defining its own pulse'*. **It does not, and it cannot** — `signal.rs` is the excitable-medium primitive (`Phase`/`Pulse`/`Field`, refractoriness, a wave across a **graph of nodes**) and produces no time series to transform. The confusion is traceable to the Additions paragraph running two sentences together: the harmonics sentence, then *'The Signal primitive Monitor relays is defined formally in §4B'* — a **cross-reference about where Signal is defined**, not a dependency. What the article says to transform is *'the fold [...] over a windowed series'*, which is the same `W = d(s,V)` series `detect.rs` already runs over and `MonitorEngine` already keeps a history of. So the window is **supplied** (`&[f64]`), exactly as `criticality.rs` takes one — **no parallel windowing is defined**, which was the real substance of the instruction. ★★ **The payoff, and it is proven not argued:** a monthly bill arriving on schedule reads as an `ExpectedCycle` and **does not escalate**, while a conformance case runs the **real `Cusum` from `detect.rs`** over the identical series and asserts it **fires**. So *'the time-domain detectors cannot do this alone'* is a measured fact about this codebase's own detector. The same magnitude arriving **off-cycle** surfaces as a `GenuineShift`, and a **new rhythm on top of the known one** still surfaces — so suppression is not a blanket mute. ★★ **A cycle must be earned twice over, and the two requirements are different:** **authorised** (`CycleProvenance::Declared` — an EVT-11 monthly period is exactly this, via `KnownCycle::from_periods` — or `Established` with enough observed cycles) **and resolvable** (the window must carry at least two full periods; a DFT bin `k` has period `N/k`). ★ **Declaration authorises a cycle; it does not make a short window able to measure it** — subtracting energy at an unresolvable bin would remove a real shift under the name of a season. Both failures return `CycleVerdict::Indeterminate` — *not* 'no cycle' and *not* 'a shift', the same third-answer family as `Skew::Unknown` and `Soundness::Unavailable`. ★★ **Testing forced a correction inspection would not have:** attributing a known cycle to one bin reported an **on-schedule bill as a shift**, because a bill is a periodic **impulse** and an impulse train puts energy at every multiple of its fundamental — which is what the row is *named* for. Fixed by attributing the harmonic series `k, 2k, 3k, …`, **with the cost named where it lives**: a genuine shift landing exactly on a harmonic of a known cycle is absorbed with it, inherent rather than a defect. ★ It **refines the existing boundary rather than opening one**: a genuine shift is classified with `detect::classify`, the same `excess → Severity` table, so it crosses via `Severity::escalates()`. A direct `O(N²)` DFT in plain `f64` — **no dependency**; an FFT is a named scaling slot. **Residuals:** ★★ **WIRED into `MonitorEngine` 2026-08-17** (the Phase-2 consolidation) — declared per sustain on `SustainWatch`, `Option` and absent by default so a sustain declaring none behaves exactly as before. ★★ **Runs per tick**, inside `ingest`, over the `W` series the chain already produces — kept rather than discarded. ★★ **And the wiring revealed a real seam, which is why integration is not the same as four passing unit suites:** `Ingested::observation()` gated on the `OodaObservation`'s **own** `escalates()`, so a detection explained by a known season would have been suppressed by `Ingested::escalates()` **and still handed to OBSERVE**. Two answers disagreeing about one reading is exactly the bug MON-13 exists to prevent; `observation()` now honours the suppression, and `suppressed_by_cycle()` reports it so a suppression is never *silent*. Still open: no window taper (leakage is not smoothed away, because a taper is another declared choice and its absence is more legible than a silent one); the mean is removed but a **linear trend deliberately is not**, since removing it silently would suppress the slow drift a Monitor most wants to see. |

**Summary:** **Monitor's detection pipeline completed 2026-08-12; the engine that OWNS it landed 2026-08-16, and with it OBSERVE became self-driving.** Two slices built the chain: `region.rs` made `urgency(s) = d(s,V)` a real quantity (MON-10), and `detect.rs` added **EWMA** (MON-5), **CUSUM** (MON-6) and the severity classification that **is** the formal watching→looking boundary. **MON-9 then supplied what was missing** — not more mathematics, but an object with a lifetime: `MonitorEngine` holds one chain per sustain, reads each sustain's **own** declared `α`/`μ₀`/`δ`/`h`/`θ_fire`, and hands an escalating reading to the OODA loop as an `OodaObservation`. Before it, the chain existed and nothing carried its state between observations; a `Watch` had to be built, fed and interpreted by whoever happened to call it. **The pipeline is the native one** — `W = d(s,V) → EWMA → CUSUM`, with **no Kalman stage**: MON-2 is a **declined import**, not a gap this engine leaves open, and MON-11's fit caveat is discharged without it. §III's *"the Monitor is always running"* is structural rather than aspirational: watching continues while the loop is parked mid-cycle, and `held_back` names the phase that declined the hand-off, so a deferred escalation is visible instead of looking like a dropped reading. Two escalation inputs, not one — a trajectory signal can fire on a dimension `V` does not bound at all, which a distance cannot measure. **Deliberately NOT stubbed:** the belief/POMDP tracker (MON-8) and the preattentive encoder (MON-7) have no placeholder fields, because an empty map reads as built-and-idle rather than absent; `tick()` stays with the host, the same boundary CTL-9 draws. **Update 2026-08-17 — MON-8 shipped, and Phase 2 opened on it** (`belief.rs`). ★★ The decisive choice was **not** following §VIII's pseudocode literally: its full-update line calls `kalman_filter.update(observation)` **by name**, and MON-8 shipped **without it** — so the declined import now has a second, concrete site rather than only this pipeline. What survives is the `point_mass`, and Sustena already had the rule for it: `Belief` **holds an EVT-10 `SnapshotDim`** and collapses through it, which makes *an observation arrived* one thing in this codebase rather than two. The behavioural proof is that **a stale reading arriving late does not collapse the belief**. ★★ Silence widens (`σ = elapsed × DRIFT_RATE`, monotonic across 24 hours) **without inventing dynamics** — `Dynamics::Unknown` holds the mean and grows only the band, because filling `predict_forward`'s shape with a fabricated model would put a made-up trajectory behind a real-looking mean. ★ And the alert **reuses the boundary without reusing the shape**: it rides `Severity::escalates()` but is not a `detect::Alert`, because that carries a `Shift` and nothing shifted — it is *we have lost sight of this*, not *this moved*. It can never be `Info`. The prior note above — *'deliberately NOT stubbed... an empty map reads as built-and-idle rather than absent'* — held exactly as intended: the tracker arrived as a real object, and **wiring it into `MonitorEngine` is named as the residual** rather than quietly assumed. **Update 2026-08-17 (second Phase 2 slice) — MON-13 shipped** (`harmonics.rs`), and it corrected its own row on the way in: the row said harmonics *consumes `signal.rs`*, and it cannot — `signal.rs` is an excitable medium over a **graph**, with no time series in it. The Additions paragraph runs the harmonics sentence and a Signal **cross-reference** together, and the row read the second as a dependency of the first. What this actually reads is the `W = d(s,V)` window `detect.rs` already runs over, **supplied** rather than re-windowed. ★★ The payoff is proven rather than argued: the **real `Cusum`** fires on the on-schedule monthly bill while the frequency-domain reading calls it an expected cycle — complement, not veto — and the same magnitude off-cycle still surfaces. ★★ A cycle must be both **authorised** and **resolvable in this window**, and failing either gives `Indeterminate` rather than a suppression: declaration does not make a short window able to measure. ★★ And testing forced the correction inspection would not have — a bill is a periodic **impulse**, so its energy sits at every multiple of its fundamental; attributing one bin reported an on-schedule bill as a shift. The fix is the harmonic series, with its cost stated: a shift on a harmonic of a known cycle is absorbed with it. **Update 2026-08-17 (third Phase 2 slice) — MON-4's typology shipped** (`windowing.rs`). ★★ The reconciliation is the substance: **tumbling is not a third mechanism** — calendar-aligned tumbling is a one-line delegate to EVT-11, and fixed-duration tumbling is **the degenerate sliding case where `step == size`**, proven by two routes producing byte-identical windows. So the genuinely new work was **sliding** and **session**. ★★ Sliding overlaps honestly (an event counted in every window containing it; `step > size` refused because it would silently drop events), and a session's end is **τ after its own last event**, with the last session left `Open` because no gap has been observed. ★★ And EVT-6's guarantee carries to the one type that could have lost it: `SessionState::close_on` takes a **`&Watermark` and nothing else**, so a clock two hours past the gap does not close a session — **a clock is not evidence**. ★ This row's `SlidingWindowAggregator` wording was corrected a second time: grep-0 **on both sides**, and deliberately unbuilt, because aggregation is a consumer of the typology rather than part of it. **Update 2026-08-17 (fourth Phase 2 slice) — MON-1 shipped, and the Monitor block of Phase 2 is COMPLETE** (`observability.rs`). ★★ The substance is a **declination plus a translation**: §I's observability matrix is declined on MON-11's ratified grounds — and with a concrete reason, that **`CAᵏ` needs a single `A` and the dynamics here is a choice, not a linear map** — while the discrete analogue is built term for term (`C` → measured dimensions, `A` → one operator's read→write edge, `CAᵏ` → `k` hops, `rank(O)=n` → every governed dimension reaches a measurement). ★★ It is **conservative where effects are unsummarised and says so**, carrying the unsummarised operators in the verdict itself — and over today's registry *every* verdict is conservative, since no shipped operator declares an `EffectSummary`. That is OP-3's finding seen from a second angle, and it is asserted rather than noted. ★ It also completes §I's implication where MON-8 started it, with the distinction kept sharp: `ungoverned()` is **runtime** (*nothing has spoken yet*), MON-1 is **structural** (*nothing ever could*). ~~**Still ⬜ in this module:** MON-7 (preattentive encoder — **moved to Phase 3**) and traces (MON-3)~~ **★★ Phase 2 moved to the Controller rows next, and CTL-11 closed them.**

**Update 2026-08-17 — MON-7 shipped, and it is the door into the surface block** (`preattentive.rs`). ★★ §VII's model maximises discriminability **subject to** the attributes coming only from Treisman's set, and the *subject to* is the load-bearing half: leaking onto a non-preattentive channel does not weaken the glance, it **ends** it. So `Channel` has exactly seven variants and **no `Other(String)`** — a non-preattentive encoding **cannot be written down**, the same discipline as EVT-6's missing `close_at(τ)`. ★★★ **Urgency is the household's, not the widget's**, and three things make that structural rather than conventional: `VisualSpec` has **no public constructor and no setters**; the channel map is **fixed, not a parameter** (a configurable map is a gaming surface); and **brightness is field-relative**, with `encode_field` the only entry point. The consequence is the property worth having — **a widget can only get brighter by being WORSE**, asserted in both directions, since a peer moving further from `V` makes it *dimmer* while it is itself unchanged. Attention is zero-sum across the field and cannot be manufactured. ★★ That **discharges UI-13 in advance**: *salience is never rendered as salience* is now a property of the encoder rather than a rule the surface has to keep. ★★ **Discriminability is RUN** — the necessary condition (distinct state-classes encode distinctly) over every class this encoder can see, with an identical peer held constant so the peer cannot be what makes them differ; `Hue` has three variants so *two near-identical hues* is unrepresentable. The psychophysical half is **named as out of reach**, not smuggled in. ★ **Encoder, not renderer** — `Hue::Amber` never `#E8A020`, asserted by checking no `#`/`px`/`rgb`/`hsl` reaches a spec; and Tufte is honoured by leaving **saturation and enclosure unassigned**, because there is no sixth or seventh data dimension and giving them one would be decoration. ★ It consumes the **real engine**: a conformance case runs a real `MonitorEngine` pass over a real `Region` and encodes its `Ingested`, so *this encodes `d(s,V)`* is checked against the thing that computes it. ★ **The counterweight belongs to the reference and it is the important one**: Slice 0 derived the single urgency signal from real data, and ★★ **Slice 13's Curated UI reused that formula verbatim** rather than inventing a second — so the reference already has **one** urgency and already feeds it to **selection**. This row adds the other consumer, the **encoding**. Two consumers, one signal, which is exactly why it cannot be gamed at either end. ★ And the contrast worth naming: `colour_rule` (`"amber_if_below_20pct"`) is a **widget-chosen** string hint, declared per field, serialised to the client and **evaluated by nothing on either side** — grep-0 in the web frontend. Even wired, it would let a widget decide when it turns amber; this row makes that shape unrepresentable. **★ MON-3 (traces) is now this module's only open row.**

**Update 2026-08-17 (the Phase-2 consolidation) — the four readings are WIRED into `MonitorEngine`.** Each of MON-8, MON-13, MON-4 and MON-1 had named *not wired into `MonitorEngine`* as its residual; all four are discharged here, **as wiring rather than new mechanism** — every module is the one it already was, reached through `Option` declarations on `SustainWatch` that leave an undeclaring sustain byte-identical to before. ★★ **What runs when is stated rather than implied, because three different answers are true:** harmonics **per tick** (it reads the `W` series the chain already produces); belief **per tick but only through `ingest_at`** (a belief needs an event time and `ingest` has none — no clock in core, so the door that carries one is explicit and `ingest` documents that it updates no belief); windowing and observability **on demand**, the latter **structural** over the operator graph. ★★ **And the wiring found a real seam, which is the argument for consolidating at all:** `observation()` gated on the `OodaObservation`'s own `escalates()`, so a detection explained by a known season would have been suppressed by `Ingested::escalates()` **and still handed to OBSERVE**. Two answers disagreeing about one reading. Fixed, with `suppressed_by_cycle()` making the suppression **visible** — a suppression nobody can see is indistinguishable from a detector that failed. ★ Belief's `SENSOR_SILENT` and harmonics' shift both cross the **existing** `Severity::escalates()` boundary; no parallel alarm channel was opened. **The spine reads: Monitor observes ✅, Tenet models ✅, Controller decides ✅ — and as of these two slices the cycle runs itself from a state update to a surfaced decision.**

#### M-TEN · Tenet — simulate + optimise  ✅ read  ★ SPINE
